| T4.132 | Queue Medical Intake/Notes — triage system text written to staffNotes instead of systemChips — The triage wizard in Queue.jsx writes system-generated text like "[Clinical Triage: CARRY-OVER]" into the staffNotes field on the appointment document. This text then renders as plain text in the Medical Intake/Notes cell (queueColumns.jsx lines 350-419) instead of as system chips/badges. The notes cell is designed with a clear separation: systemChips[] renders as colored Chip badges at the top (EMERGENCY, CARRY-OVER, NO-SHOW, etc.), while staffNotes/clientNotes render as plain text below. But the triage wizard bypasses systemChips and embeds system labels in staffNotes as "[Clinical Triage: X]" strings. Fix: (1) Find all triage wizard write paths in Queue.jsx that write to staffNotes with "[Clinical Triage: ...]" format. Change them to write to systemChips via arrayUnion instead. The triage label (CARRY-OVER, etc.) should be a systemChips entry, not a staffNotes string. (2) If the triage wizard also writes genuine staff notes alongside the label, split them: system label → systemChips, actual notes → staffNotes. (3) The staffNotes field should contain ONLY human-written notes from staff, never system-generated labels. (4) Consider a one-time cleanup for existing appointments with "[Clinical Triage: ...]" in staffNotes — either a migration script or dual-read fallback that detects the pattern and renders it as a chip. | P2 | 1.5 hrs | — | DONE | Removed triagePrefix from staffNotes at both carry-over write sites (EOD batch + inline). systemChips already written correctly — CARRY-OVER label now renders as a chip badge only, not duplicated as "[Clinical Triage: ...]" text in notes. triagePrefix declarations preserved for pulse event audit context. |
| T4.133 | Admin page header unification — consistent 2-row layout, button hierarchy, search bar, and responsive wrapping across all 12 pages — Every admin page header was built independently with ad-hoc toolbars: inconsistent title casing (mixed case "Patient Queue" vs uppercase "SERVICES"), inconsistent primary action colors (red, blue, brown, orange across pages), mixed button styles (5 contained buttons on Dashboard alone), different search bar variants (filled on Services, outlined elsewhere), subtitles on some pages (Dashboard, Forensic Reports) but not others, filters sometimes inline with titles and sometimes below, 11+ toolbar items on Inventory. Fix by applying a unified PageHeader pattern to all 12 admin pages. NO LOGIC CHANGES — only layout, styling, and text. Decisions locked: (1) Titles: all uppercase, max 2 words (DASHBOARD, QUEUE, RECORDS, PATIENTS, SERVICES, INVENTORY, STAFF, TRANSACTIONS, EXPENSES, SETTINGS, REPORTS, NOTIFICATIONS). (2) Primary action: Sky Blue #3ABEF9 contained for the ONE main action per page (+ NEW SERVICE, + ADD ITEM, etc). (3) Button hierarchy: primary=contained Sky Blue, secondary=outlined, destructive=contained Red (CLEAR QUEUE only), tertiary=icon-only with tooltip. (4) Search bar: all outlined variant, flex:1 with maxWidth:350px minWidth:180px, SearchIcon startAdornment, placeholder "Search {noun}...". (5) Subtitles: remove all (Dashboard "Starbarks Veterinary Clinic", Forensic Reports subtitle). (6) Layout: Row 1 = title + record count badge + primary action button. Row 2 = search + filters + dropdowns + toggles with flexWrap:'wrap' for responsive flow. (7) KPI cards: keep current per-page placement (no change). (8) Responsive: Row 2 uses flexWrap:'wrap' + gap:1 so controls flow to next line on smaller screens — combined with sidebar collapse (shipped in T4.131), pages work down to 768px. Pages to update (12): Dashboard.jsx, Queue.jsx, Records.jsx, Patients.jsx, Services.jsx (parent), Inventory.jsx, Staff.jsx (parent), Sales.jsx, Expenses.jsx, Settings.jsx, ForensicReports.jsx, NotificationLogs.jsx. Purely visual/CSS — zero Firestore writes, zero hook changes, zero state changes, zero functional logic changes. | P2 | 5-6 hrs | — | DONE | COLORS.sky + skyHover tokens. All 12 admin pages unified: 2-word uppercase titles, Sky Blue primary actions, outlined search bars with flex sizing, 2-row layout with flexWrap, subtitles + title icons removed. Settings save stays green. Patients exempt (CRM layout). 13 files, zero logic changes. UX consistency — 12 pages should look like they belong to the same application. The unified 2-row template with flexWrap is inherently responsive without per-page breakpoint logic. |
| T4.134 | Queue transparency system — department lanes, time-based wait estimates, status breadcrumb, explainer, colored badges, department-filtered position — VetConnect's queue is a D/M/c priority-capable service queue with concurrent department routing, not strict FIFO. Pets in different departments (Grooming vs Veterinary Medicine) are served simultaneously by different staff, so a later ticket may finish before an earlier one. This confuses clients who expect strict number order. Fix with 6 changes: (1) Department lanes: replace the single flat queue list on the Lobby Monitor (Monitor.jsx) and mobile QueueScreen with department-grouped lanes showing "Grooming (2 waiting)" and "Consultation (3 waiting)" as parallel columns/sections. Clients see their position within their department, not the global queue. (2) Time-based wait estimate (absorbs T4.6): calculate estimated wait per department using avgConsultDuration × patientsAheadInSameDept ÷ staffInDept. Display "Estimated wait: ~20 min" on mobile QueueScreen instead of just "X patients ahead." Uses department-specific average from completed consults today. (3) Status breadcrumb on QueueScreen: add a horizontal breadcrumb below the queue position showing "Checked in → Waiting → With the vet → Pharmacy → Checkout → Done" with the current stage highlighted. Reuse the VisitTimeline pattern from T4.86 (buildVisitTimeline.js + CLIENT_LABEL_MAP) adapted as a horizontal breadcrumb. (4) "Why was I skipped?" explainer: add an info (ℹ️) IconButton on QueueScreen that shows a bottom sheet or Alert explaining "Pets may be seen in a different order based on the type of service. Grooming, veterinary consultations, and vaccinations are handled by different staff simultaneously." Dismissible, shown once. (5) Department-colored ticket badges on Lobby Monitor: the "NOW SERVING" display adds the department name + color badge: "NOW SERVING: A-003 — Grooming 🟢". Department colors from the departments collection. (6) "Ahead in your lane" department-filtered count: mobile QueueScreen and ClientDashboard change "X patients ahead of you" to "X patients ahead in {department}" — filtered to the same department as the client's appointment. Files: Monitor.jsx (department lanes + colored badges), QueueScreen.js (department lanes + time estimate + breadcrumb + explainer + filtered count), ClientDashboard.js (filtered count), Queue.jsx or useDashboardData (avg consult duration per dept). Absorbs T4.6 (real-time capacity forecasting). | P2 | 6-7 hrs | T2.281 | DONE | 6 approaches shipped: dept-filtered ahead count (QueueScreen + ClientDashboard), per-dept time estimate (absorbs T4.6), dept lane header with colored dot, status breadcrumb (6 stages + on-hold/confined annotation), explainer Alert, Monitor dept badges + grouped upcoming lanes. 3 files. Department lanes + time estimates + breadcrumb + explainer eliminate the "why was I skipped?" confusion. Same model as hospital outpatient department displays. |
| T4.135 | Multi-channel notification system — email (Resend) + SMS (Semaphore) fallback cascade with automated triggers — The current notification system is push-only via Cloudflare Worker + Expo Push API. If the client's phone has no internet, app is uninstalled, or they're on a different device, notifications are lost. Add two fallback channels: (1) Email via Resend API (free tier: 100 emails/day, sufficient for small clinic). Add POST /email endpoint to Cloudflare Worker. Resend API key stored as Worker env var. HTML email templates matching the 17 existing push templates (12 status + 2 vaccine + 3 appointment). Email sent to the user's registered email (required field from T4.128 registration). (2) SMS via Semaphore API (PH-native gateway, ₱0.35-0.50/SMS). Add POST /sms endpoint to Cloudflare Worker. Semaphore API key stored as Worker env var. SMS is SELECTIVE — only critical notifications: appointment confirmation, tomorrow reminder, today reminder (3 templates). Other notifications (status changes, vaccine reminders) use push + email only to control costs. (3) Automated cascade in sendPushNotification.js: every existing push trigger (18 write paths across 6 files) automatically tries push → email → SMS (for critical types). No manual staff action. The _dispatchPush function already fires on every status change — extend it to call /email after /push, and /sms for critical types. (4) Per-channel logging: notification_log entries get a channel field ('push', 'email', 'sms') so the admin NotificationLogs page can filter by delivery channel. (5) Settings toggle: enableEmailNotifications (default true) + enableSmsNotifications (default false, admin must opt-in and configure Semaphore API key) in clinic_settings. (6) Worker reference copy update: VetConnect-Backend/cloudflare-worker/worker.js updated with /email and /sms endpoints. Worker env vars needed: RESEND_API_KEY + SEMAPHORE_API_KEY (in addition to existing ANTHROPIC_API_KEY + FIREBASE_API_KEY). (7) Guest walk-in clients: have phone but no email (email optional in WalkInModal). Their cascade is push → SMS only. Registered users have both email + phone guaranteed from T4.128. Files: Cloudflare Worker (2 new endpoints), sendPushNotification.js (_dispatchPush cascade), notificationTemplateConstants.js (email HTML templates + SMS text templates), Settings.jsx (email/SMS toggles + API key fields), NotificationLogs.jsx (channel filter chip), Worker reference copy. No Blaze needed — all via Cloudflare Worker. | P2 | 8-10 hrs | T4.90, T4.91 | DONE | Multi-channel notification cascade: push (Expo) → email (Resend, free 100/day) → SMS (Semaphore, PH-native, selective critical only). 3-channel fire-and-forget in _dispatchPush, zero caller changes to 18 existing sendPushNotification call sites. Cron handlers send email for all reminders + SMS for tomorrow/today appointment reminders. Settings toggles (email default on, SMS default off). NotificationLogs channel column + filter. SendNotificationDialog email-independent of push. Worker /email + /sms endpoints. 8 files. Thesis differentiator — multi-channel notification with automated fallback cascade. Push (free) → Email (free via Resend) → SMS (₱0.40/msg via Semaphore, selective). Same architecture as commercial vet apps (Petdesk, PetPage). Panelists see professional-grade notification infrastructure. |

| T4.136 | Admin forgot password — sendPasswordResetEmail on Login.jsx — The admin Login.jsx has no password recovery flow. Staff who forget their credentials have no self-service reset path — they must ask the clinic admin to create a new account or manually reset in Firebase Console. Fix: add a "Forgot Password?" link below the password field on VetConnect-Admin/src/pages/Login.jsx. Clicking opens a MUI Dialog with an email TextField + "Send Reset Link" Button. On submit, call sendPasswordResetEmail(auth, email) from Firebase Auth. Handle errors: auth/user-not-found → "No account found", auth/invalid-email → "Invalid email", success → "Reset link sent to your email." Dialog closes on success. Firebase handles the reset email automatically (customizable in Firebase Console → Authentication → Templates). Same pattern as mobile LoginScreen forgot password. Single file: Login.jsx. No Blaze needed. | P1 | 20 min | — | DONE | Shipped in session 2026-05-01/04. Forgot Password dialog on Login.jsx with sendPasswordResetEmail. |
| T4.137 | Force password change on first login — mustChangePassword enforcement — T2.208 sets mustChangePassword: true on the user doc when admin creates a staff account with a temp password. But nothing checks this flag on login — staff can use the temp password forever. Fix: (1) In App.jsx (admin), after the auth state resolves and user doc is fetched, check if userData.mustChangePassword === true. If so, redirect to a new ChangePasswordScreen (or show a MUI Dialog) that requires the user to set a new password before accessing the dashboard. (2) The ChangePassword flow: current password field (pre-filled or not) + new password + confirm password. On submit: reauthenticateWithCredential(auth.currentUser, credential) → updatePassword(auth.currentUser, newPassword) → updateDoc(userRef, { mustChangePassword: false }) → navigate to Dashboard. (3) The dialog/screen is BLOCKING — no way to dismiss or navigate away without changing the password. Uses Firebase Auth updatePassword() — no Blaze needed. Files: App.jsx (redirect check) + new ChangePasswordDialog component or inline in App.jsx. | P1 | 1 hr | T2.208 | DONE | Blocking Dialog in App.jsx: non-dismissible (no Escape, no backdrop, no close btn), reauthenticateWithCredential → updatePassword → updateDoc(mustChangePassword:false), onSnapshot closes dialog automatically. 3 TextFields + visibility toggles + Enter key submits + CircularProgress loading state + full error map. Inline in AppShell, zero new components. |
| T4.138 | Staff revocation Auth block without Blaze — Firestore rule + client-side signOut — T3.40 (Firebase Auth disable on staff revocation) requires Blaze for admin.auth().updateUser(). Workaround on Spark plan: (1) Firestore rules: update isStaff() helper in firestore.rules to also check disabled !== true: return isAuth() && getUserRole().role in ['admin','staff','veterinarian','groomer'] && getUserRole().disabled != true. This blocks ALL Firestore reads/writes for disabled staff — even with a valid Auth token, they can't access any data. (2) Client-side enforcement: in App.jsx (admin), after fetching the user doc on auth state change, check if userData.disabled === true. If so, call auth.signOut() immediately and show an Alert/Snackbar: "Your account has been deactivated. Contact the clinic administrator." (3) Mobile App.js: same check — if the user doc has disabled: true, sign out and show message. This provides defense-in-depth: Firestore rules block data access (server-side), client-side signOut prevents UI access. The only remaining gap is that the Firebase Auth token itself isn't invalidated (a technically sophisticated person could still authenticate) — but with zero Firestore access, the token is useless. Files: firestore.rules (isStaff update), App.jsx (admin disabled check), App.js (mobile disabled check). Deploy firestore rules after. | P2 | 1 hr | — | DONE | firestore.rules: added disabled != true guard to isStaff() and isAdmin() (uses != true not == false to handle missing field). App.jsx: useEffect detects profile.disabled === true in real time via onSnapshot, calls signOut + shows red Snackbar outside isValidStaff guard so it persists after redirect. Mobile App.js disabled check at lines 70-71 confirmed complete — no changes needed. |
| T4.139 | Booking engine intelligence upgrade — per-pet weight-resolved pricing, per-pet service selection, cumulative capacity check, parallel department scheduling — The mobile booking engine (useBookingEngine.js + BookAppointment.js) has 4 gaps that keep it at Intermediate tier: (1) Price summary bug (BookAppointment.js line 1482): displays flat service.price × selectedPets.length instead of calculating per-pet weight-resolved totals via resolveTieredPrice(). A 5kg dog (₱400 grooming) and 30kg dog (₱700 grooming) both show the flat rate. Fix: replace the reduce with a nested loop: sum(resolveTieredPrice(service, pet.weight) for each service for each pet). Remove the "Final price adjusted at checkout" disclaimer — the preview should be accurate. (2) Global services (BookAppointment.js line 67): selectedServices is a single array applied to ALL pets. Can't book "Dog A: Vaccination only, Dog B: Grooming + Vaccination." Fix: mirror WalkInModal's petEntries[i].selectedServices pattern — restructure the booking flow so Step 2 (service selection) becomes per-pet. Each pet card shows its own service picker. Species filtering applies per-pet (Dog A sees dog services, Cat B sees cat services). The slot generator sums all selected services across all pets for total duration. (3) Cumulative capacity check (useBookingEngine.js lines 310-327): each pet's capacity check runs independently without accumulating concurrent demand. 3 pets needing Surgery (capacity: 1) all pass because each sees 0 overlaps. Fix: during the per-pet loop, track a running count of "virtual bookings" from previous pets in the same group. Add these to the overlap count before comparing against capacity. (4) Parallel department scheduling (useBookingEngine.js lines 236-241): all services are sequential (duration summed). Two services in different departments (Grooming + Vaccination) could run in parallel. Fix: group services by department, find the longest department duration as the total (max instead of sum for cross-department services). Same-department services remain sequential. Files: BookAppointment.js (price summary, per-pet services UI, step 2 restructure), useBookingEngine.js (cumulative capacity, parallel scheduling). | P1 | 7-8 hrs | — | DONE | Booking engine Professional tier: petServiceMap per-pet service selection (single-pet unchanged, multi-pet gets per-pet pickers + Apply to All), weight-resolved price summary with per-pet breakdown, cumulative capacity with virtual bookings accumulator, parallel dept scheduling (max across depts, sum within), time-based JIT stagger refinement. 2 files, zero Firestore schema changes, 14 verified scenarios. Thesis-critical — the booking engine is the #1 feature panelists will test. Per-pet services + accurate pricing + cumulative capacity + parallel scheduling move it from Intermediate to Professional tier. Exceeds commercial vet apps (PetDesk, Vetstoria) which only support single-pet single-service booking. |
| T4.140 | Admin Queue validation parity — capacity check on confirm, stagger time display, weight delta warning, department capacity strip — The admin Queue page (Queue.jsx + queueColumns.jsx) faithfully DISPLAYS everything the booking engine creates (multi-pet groups, services, departments) but doesn't VALIDATE the same rules when staff manually manage appointments. 4 gaps: (1) No capacity validation on confirm/accept (Queue.jsx lines 3696-3717): when staff confirms a pending appointment, the Queue only checks "does ANY staff exist in this department" — not "is this department currently at capacity." A department at capacity can accept more appointments. Fix: before confirming, query today's appointments for the same department + time window, count overlaps, compare against departmentCapacity[dept]. Reject with MUI Dialog if at capacity: "Cannot confirm — {dept} is at capacity ({N}/{N} active). Reschedule or wait for a slot." (2) Stagger time display (queueColumns.jsx Triage Clock column): multi-pet groups show the same scheduled time for all pets. The booking engine staggers them (Pet 1 at 2:00, Pet 2 at 2:30) but the Queue doesn't display the offset. Fix: show per-pet actual start time in the Triage Clock column. For group members with groupIndex > 0, calculate: scheduledDate + (groupIndex × bundleTotalMinutes). Show "APPT: 2:30 PM (Pet 2/3)" instead of the shared base time. (3) Weight delta warning on arrival (queueColumns.jsx identity card or Clinical Passport popover): if a pet's arrival weight (from check-in or vitals) differs significantly from the booked weight (stored on the appointment), show a warning: "Booked at 5kg, arrived 8kg — pricing may change." Threshold: ±20% weight change. Display as an amber chip or tooltip in the Clinical Passport popover. (4) Department capacity KPI strip (Queue.jsx header area): add a row of small department capacity indicators below the status tabs: "Grooming: 1/1 busy · Veterinary: 2/3 available · Vaccination: 0/2 idle." Computed from today's in-consult/dispensing appointments grouped by serviceCategory vs departmentCapacity. Helps staff see at a glance which departments have room before accepting walk-ins or confirming pending appointments. Files: Queue.jsx (capacity validation on confirm + dept capacity strip), queueColumns.jsx (stagger time display + weight delta warning). | P2 | 4 hrs | T4.139 | TODO | Admin parity — the Queue's display is professional but its validation is Amateur-Intermediate. These 4 fixes bring validation to Professional tier, matching the booking engine's intelligence. Prevents manual overbooking, surfaces timing conflicts, and alerts staff to pricing-relevant weight changes. |

| T4.141 | Assessment quadrant structured diagnosis system — diagnosis catalog, Autocomplete form, severity/staging, free-text clinical notes, statistics unlocked — The Assessment quadrant in ClinicalWorkspace currently uses a single free-text TextField (SoapGrid.jsx) where vets type diagnoses as unstructured prose ("Chronic kidney disease stage 2, dental grade 3, possible UTI"). This prevents accurate diagnosis statistics (Dashboard T2.290 "Top 5 Diagnoses" uses fuzzy string matching producing duplicates like "CKD" vs "Chronic Kidney Disease" vs "chronic kidney dz"), species-specific prevalence tracking, trend analysis, and problem list integration (T4.13). | T4.149 | POS custom discount system — per-transaction + per-item discounts with percentage and fixed amount support. Applies to the subtotal AFTER per-item discounts. (2) Per-item discount: a small "disc" button per cart row that opens an inline % or ₱ field. Per-item discounts reduce the line total BEFORE the transaction discount. (3) Visual stacking preview: totals section shows the full breakdown (Subtotal → Item discounts → After items → Bill discount → Total) so the cashier sees exactly how both layers combine. (4) "2 discounts applied" info chip when both layers active. (5) Audit: every discount logged on the sale doc (discountType, discountValue, discountScope, discountReason, discountedBy). | T4.153 | POS sequential receipt numbering — replace random Firestore doc ID slice with sequential OR numbers. (1) Create a Firestore counter doc at counters/receipt_sequence with a numeric value field. (2) Inside POSModal's checkout runTransaction, atomically increment the counter and use the result as the receipt number. Format: OR-{YYYYMMDD}-{zero-padded sequential} (e.g., OR-20260504-0023). (3) The counter runs globally (not per-day reset) to guarantee no gaps — BIR requires unbroken sequence. Per-day numbering would require a reset mechanism that could miss if no transaction happens on a day. (4) Store receiptNumber on the sales doc. Display on receipt header + Sales ledger. (5) Firestore rules: counters collection — allow read/write to isStaff(). Files: POSModal.jsx (counter read + increment inside transaction), Sales.jsx (display), firestore.rules (counters collection). New Firestore doc: counters/receipt_sequence. | P2 | 30 min | — | DONE | Random receipt numbers fail BIR audit for registered businesses. Sequential numbering is professional POS standard. |

| T4.142 | Inventory 3-tier product classification system + category manager redesign — Replace the binary isMedicine toggle with a 3-tier productClass system (Medicine, Medical Supply, Retail) that drives dispensing routing, auto-dosing, discharge sections, and field visibility. Full category manager redesign with table layout and edit dialogs. 20 changes across 10 files: TIER SYSTEM (core): (1) Data model: new productClass field on inventory docs ('medicine', 'medical_supply', 'retail'). Dual-write: also write isMedicine: productClass === 'medicine' for backward compat. Readers check productClass first, fall back to isMedicine for legacy docs. (2) ProductFormModal.jsx: replace isMedicine toggle with productClass Select dropdown + dynamic helper text per tier. MEDICINE: "Health product prescribed or recommended by the vet. Routes to dispensing for verification. Auto-generates dosing instructions. Appears in discharge medications." MEDICAL SUPPLY: "Take-home clinical supply. Goes directly to billing. Appears in discharge as take-home supplies." RETAIL: "Non-clinical product. Goes directly to billing. Does not appear on discharge summary." (3) Category defaults: each category gets a default productClass. medicine→medicine, vaccine→medicine, supplies→medical_supply, food→retail, accessories→retail, lab→medical_supply. New products inherit from category, per-item override via the Select. (4) Tier effect matrix: MEDICINE: dispensing routing ✅, auto-dosing (sig) ✅, instructions always-visible ✅, discharge "Medications" ✅, SC/PWD per-item toggle. MEDICAL SUPPLY: dispensing ❌, auto-dosing ❌, instructions collapsible, discharge "Take-Home Supplies" ✅, SC/PWD ❌. RETAIL: dispensing ❌, auto-dosing ❌, instructions collapsible, discharge ❌, SC/PWD ❌. CONDITIONAL FIELDS (ProductFormModal): (5) Rename "Unit of Measure" → "Dispensing Unit". Helper text: "What the client receives as one unit. Used in dosing instructions: '1 capsule twice daily.'" Required for Medicine, optional for Medical Supply + Retail. (6) Dosage/Strength: shown only for Medicine tier. Hidden for Supply + Retail. Optional even when shown. (7) Lot Number + Expiry Date: shown only for Medicine tier. Hidden for Supply + Retail. Optional — batch tracking activates based on data presence, not tier. CATEGORY MANAGER (InventoryCategoryManager.jsx): (8) Remove "drug" from the 7 default categories → 6 defaults. Add migration logic: if default_drug doc exists, batch-update all items with category==='drug' to 'medicine', delete default_drug doc. Idempotent. (9) Replace chip-based display with MUI Table: columns Name, Classification (tier chip with color: Medicine=red, Supply=gray, Retail=neutral), Items (active count from inventory prop), System (lock icon for default_ docs), Actions (Edit + Delete IconButtons). (10) Replace inline quick-add with Add Category MUI Dialog: name TextField + productClass Select (with same helper text as ProductFormModal) + description (optional). (11) Add Edit Category Dialog (reuse Add Dialog pre-filled). On name change: batch-update all inventory items where category === oldName. On productClass change: batch-update all items that don't have a per-item productClass override. Default categories: name locked (disabled), productClass toggleable, delete blocked. (12) Item count: computed client-side from inventory array passed as prop. No extra Firestore query. CLINICAL WORKSPACE (ClinicalWorkspace.jsx): (13) Dispensing routing: change hasDrugsInCart from treatmentCart.some(item => item.isDrug) to treatmentCart.some(item => (item.productClass || (item.isDrug ? 'medicine' : 'retail')) === 'medicine'). Dual-read fallback for legacy cart items. (14) Auto-dosing (sig): only generate sig-based instructions for productClass === 'medicine'. Medical Supply + Retail get collapsible manual instructions (existing pattern). (15) Discharge summary write at sign-off: split the current medications-only write into two arrays. dischargeSummary.medications: treatmentCart.filter(productClass === 'medicine'). NEW dischargeSummary.supplies: treatmentCart.filter(productClass === 'medical_supply'). Retail items excluded from discharge. ADMIN DISPLAY (PatientDashboard.jsx): (16) Record expanded view: replace isDrug/!isDrug 2-section split with 3-section split: "Rx" (medicine) + "Take-Home Supplies" (medical_supply). Retail items shown in a muted "Other" section or hidden. Discharge block: add supplies section reading from dischargeSummary.supplies[]. (17) Prescriptions sidebar widget: filter by productClass === 'medicine' instead of isDrug. (18) Dedup: when dischargeSummary exists, hide standalone items section on PatientDashboard. Going-Home block shows both medications + supplies. MOBILE DISPLAY (PetHistoryScreen.js): (19) Record expanded view: replace isDrug/!isDrug split ("PRESCRIBED MEDICATIONS" / "OTHER ITEMS") with productClass-based split. When dischargeSummary exists, hide standalone items entirely — Going-Home Instructions is the single source showing both 💊 MEDICATIONS + 🩹 TAKE-HOME SUPPLIES sections. For legacy records without discharge, show items grouped by productClass. QUEUE + POS: (20) DispensingVerificationDialog: change labels from "DRUG"/"PRODUCT" to "MEDICINE"/"SUPPLY"/"RETAIL" with color-coded badges. Queue columns dispensing badge: productClass === 'medicine' instead of isDrug. POSModal: SC/PWD eligibility stays per-item toggle (isScPwdEligible) — independent of tier. Decisions locked: 3 tiers (Medicine/Medical Supply/Retail), dual-write data model, Select + helper text UI, dispensing routing for Medicine only, auto-dosing for Medicine only, two discharge sections, "Dispensing Unit" rename, conditional fields (unit/dosage/lot/expiry shown for Medicine only, all optional), category table + edit dialog, "drug" category removed, mobile dedup (Going-Home as single source). Files: ProductFormModal.jsx, InventoryCategoryManager.jsx, Inventory.jsx, ClinicalWorkspace.jsx, PatientDashboard.jsx, PetHistoryScreen.js, useInventory.js, Queue columns (queueColumns.jsx), DispensingVerificationDialog.jsx, POSModal.jsx. | P1 | 6-8 hrs | — | DONE | 3-tier productClass system (Medicine/Medical Supply/Retail) across 10 files. | T4.166 | PetHistoryScreen record content layout redesign — diagnosis-first hierarchy, header compaction, department bottom sheet, year dropdown + month chips, Subjective visible, global AI FAB — The T4.155 redesign fixed navigation/collapse/timeline but the CONTENT inside expanded records is disorganized with no visual hierarchy, the header wastes 3 vertical rows, department chips don't scale, and the month picker doesn't handle multi-year histories. 12 changes: (1) Content hierarchy reorder: Diagnosis hero (large bold text, multi-line for multi-dx) → Status + Prognosis (one merged line: "Stable · Prognosis: Good") → "REASON FOR VISIT" (Subjective text, now visible to pet owners — reverses T2.8 for Subjective only. Objective stays hidden. Plan stays hidden — replaced by Client Instructions in Going-Home.) → Going-Home Instructions (visually dominant) → Vitals (all 7) → Actions. (2) Collapsed record header simplified: date + vet name only. Diagnosis and status badge move inside expanded content. (3) Header compaction: merge the two back buttons + pet name into one header bar: "← LULUW'S CHART  4 records". Remove the separate "MEDICAL RECORDS" header bar and the "< PET NAME'S CHART" row. Saves 2 vertical rows — records appear above the fold. (4) Expand All moved into search field: expand/collapse toggle becomes an icon button (⇕) inside the search TextField's endAdornment. One row saved. (5) Department filter: replace horizontal chips (ALL, GENERAL, GROOMING, VACCINATION) with a filter icon button (🔽) next to search → opens a bottom sheet with checkboxes per department + item count: "☑ General (8) / ☑ Grooming (3) / ☐ Vaccination (2)". Multi-select — pet owner can filter to "Grooming + Dental" simultaneously. CLEAR ALL + APPLY FILTER buttons. Filter icon shows badge count when active. Scales to unlimited departments. Remove chip row entirely. (6) Month picker upgrade: replace flat horizontal month chips with year dropdown + month chips. One dropdown for year [2026 ▼], then month chips for that year only. Switching year refreshes the chips. Year dropdown only appears when records span 2+ years. For single-year pets, no dropdown — just month chips (identical to current T4.155 behavior). Default shows most recent year. Tap month → scroll FlatList. Scroll FlatList → update active month. Bidirectional sync preserved. (7) Subjective now visible to pet owners: show owner's words with label "REASON FOR VISIT" in a subtle container. Not shown for records where subjective is empty (grooming-only visits). (8) Vitals: all 7 fields shown in a compact grid (4+3 or 3+4 layout). Measured vitals show values with units. Missing vitals show "not taken" in muted style (COLORS.textMuted, italic). No "—" dashes. (9) Going-Home Instructions visually dominant: 2px border (COLORS.success), offset shadow (SHADOW.record), light tinted background, 📋 icon in header. Contents per T4.142 + T4.158: status pill, diagnosis, "WHAT TO DO" (client instructions), 💊 MEDICATIONS section, 🩹 TAKE-HOME SUPPLIES section (medical_supply tier), recheck interval, follow-up date with Book Follow-Up button, CALL US button, signed by vet name. (10) ASK AI: remove per-record ASK AI buttons. Add ONE global floating action button (FAB) at bottom-right of screen. FAB always visible during scroll. AI receives full pet history context regardless of which record is expanded. (11) Actions: replace full-width DOWNLOAD VISIT SUMMARY button with compact icon row below Going-Home (📄 Download + 🔗 Share icons). Saves vertical space. (12) Diagnosis visual treatment: when diagnoses exist, render as large bold text (fontSize 20, fontWeight 900, COLORS.brand). Multi-diagnosis renders each on its own line. Severity shown as inline text: "CHRONIC KIDNEY DISEASE (STAGE III)". When no diagnoses (grooming visit after T4.164), no hero section — the record shows service chips + vitals only. Decisions locked: A (diagnosis-first hierarchy), A (large bold diagnosis), "REASON FOR VISIT" (subjective label), all 7 vitals with "not taken", C (Going-Home visually dominant), B (global AI FAB), C (department filter bottom sheet), F (year dropdown + month chips). (13) Record card background: white (#FFFFFF) for all clinical content (diagnosis hero, reason for visit, vitals, actions). The cream background (COLORS.cream #FFF8E1) is reserved ONLY for the discharge section — creates a natural visual break signaling "this is the actionable take-home part." No border or box treatment on the discharge section — the color shift itself IS the separator. (14) Discharge section full-width: remove the current box-within-box styling (borderWidth, borderColor, borderLeftWidth, marginTop on dischargeCard). Replace with full-width cream section using negative horizontal margin to extend edge-to-edge within the record card: marginHorizontal: -16 (cancel parent padding) + paddingHorizontal: 16 (re-add inside). | T4.167 | Admin PatientDashboard record view redesign — SOAP-order layout, Assessment hero with per-dx notes input, collapsible Objective, compact vitals, header actions, service overflow, dedup, discharge rename — The expanded medical record view in PatientDashboard is a flat vertical list with no visual hierarchy, diagnosis shown 3 times, Plan and Going-Home show identical text, vitals split across two locations, Amendment button buried, per-diagnosis notes have no input UI, Plan has an inconsistent green accent. 13 changes: SOAP-ORDER LAYOUT: (1) Content follows standard SOAP sequence that vets are trained on: S → O → A (hero) → P → Discharge Notes. The mobile app (T4.166) puts diagnosis first for pet owners ("what's wrong?"). The admin puts it in SOAP position for vets (clinical reading convention). Same data, different order for different audiences. SUBJECTIVE (S): (2) Label: "SUBJECTIVE" (standard SOAP term). Content: soapData.subjective (auto-populated from intake notes via T4.158 + vet's interview additions). Identical text on both admin and mobile — no confidential info in Subjective (owner was present for all of it). Mobile labels it "REASON FOR VISIT" for pet owners. OBJECTIVE (O): (3) Collapsible: physical exam text ("WNL: General Appearance, EENT...") is verbose and rarely re-read. Default collapsed with "OBJECTIVE" header + expand toggle. | T4.168 | POS checkout transaction restructure — fix read-after-write violation in runTransaction — The handleCheckout runTransaction (POSModal.jsx lines 710-920) violates Firestore's "all reads before all writes" constraint. The deductInventoryInTransaction helper (lines 629-699) loops through cart products doing READ inventory → WRITE inventory update → WRITE inventory log PER ITEM. With 2+ products, the second item's transaction.get() happens AFTER the first item's transaction.update() + transaction.set(). Additionally, the receipt counter read (line 732) happens AFTER all inventory writes regardless of cart size. This causes transaction failures ("Firestore transactions require all reads to be executed before all writes") on any checkout with products. Fix: restructure the entire checkout transaction into two phases. PHASE 1 (ALL READS): (a) Read all appointment docs (individual or group — lines 716-721), (b) Read ALL inventory items for all product cart items in one loop (collect itemDoc.data() into a Map, NO writes), (c) Read receipt counter (line 732). PHASE 2 (ALL COMPUTATIONS): (d) Compute FIFO batch deductions per product from the collected data (currently done inside the read loop — extract into pure computation), (e) Compute receipt number from counter value, (f) Build sale doc payload, (g) Build appointment update payloads. PHASE 3 (ALL WRITES): (h) Write inventory updates (stock deduction + batch array), (i) Write inventory logs (one per product), (j) Write receipt counter update, (k) Write sale doc, (l) Write appointment updates (status:completed, statusHistory, balanceRemaining, clinicalPulse). The deductInventoryInTransaction helper must be split: deductInventoryReads(transaction, cartItems) returns a Map of inventory data, deductInventoryCompute(cartItems, inventoryDataMap) returns computed update payloads + batchSourceMap, deductInventoryWrites(transaction, computedPayloads) applies all writes. Both the group billing branch and individual billing branch must follow the same phase ordering. The FIFO batch computation currently modifies the batches array in-place during the loop — extract into a pure function that takes the batch array + qty and returns the new batch array + batchSource without mutating. Test scenarios: single product, 2+ products, services-only (no products — zero inventory reads), group billing (multiple appointment reads), first-ever receipt (counter doc doesn't exist — bootstrap with transaction.set). Files: POSModal.jsx (transaction restructure). | P1 | 1.5-2 hrs | — | DONE | Transaction restructured into 3 strict phases: Phase 1 (all reads: appointments + inventory + receipt counter), Phase 2 (all computation: FIFO batch deduction via pure computeFifoBatchDeduction, stock validation, payload assembly), Phase 3 (all writes: inventory updates + logs + counter + sale doc + appointment updates). Old deductInventoryInTransaction deleted, replaced by readInventoryDocs + computeInventoryDeductions + writeInventoryUpdates. Zero transaction.get() after first transaction.update(). All sale doc + appointment update fields preserved identically. Build clean, 322/322 tests pass. |
| T4.169 | Reservation audit logging — reserveStock and releaseStock in useInventory.js (~lines 252-280) perform transactional updates to the reserved field but never write to inventory_logs. Staff seeing reserved=5 on a product have no way to trace WHO reserved WHEN from which consult. Fix: add logEvent() calls inside both functions after the transaction commits. reserveStock logs action:'RESERVE' with { productId, productName, qty, appointmentId, staffName, timestamp }. releaseStock logs action:'RELEASE' with the same shape. Add 'RESERVE' and 'RELEASE' to GlobalActivityLog ACTION_CONFIG (normalizeInventoryLog.js) with appropriate icons and colors (RESERVE=blue/LockIcon, RELEASE=green/LockOpenIcon). POSModal's direct reserved decrement (~line 680) should also log action:'RELEASE' using the same shape — add an inventory_logs addDoc inside the existing transaction after the inventory update write. This gives full audit trail visibility: staff can filter Activity Log by RESERVE/RELEASE to see all reservation activity, diagnose "why is this product showing 5 reserved?", and verify that checkout properly released. | P2 | 20 min | — | TODO | Audit visibility — reservation activity is currently invisible in inventory_logs. Completes the reservation system's accountability story. |
| T4.170 | Stranded reservation cleanup — browser crash recovery via dual reconciliation — If a vet's browser crashes or tab closes while the useEffect cleanup in ClinicalWorkspace is blocked (e.g., network offline), reserved quantities stay inflated indefinitely. No Cloud Functions are deployed (Spark plan) to reconcile. Fix with two layers: (1) Inventory page auto-reconcile on mount: when Inventory.jsx mounts, run a one-shot reconciliation — query all appointments with status in active consult statuses (in-consult, on-hold, dispensing, billing), collect all product IDs and quantities from their encounterItems/treatmentCart, sum reserved-per-product from legitimate active consults, compare against actual reserved values on inventory docs. For any product where actual reserved > legitimate sum, silently reset reserved to the legitimate value via updateDoc. Log each correction as action:'RECONCILE' in inventory_logs with { productId, oldReserved, newReserved, reason:'Stranded reservation cleanup' }. Fire-and-forget, no UI — just runs on mount. (2) ClinicalWorkspace mount piggyback: before reserveStock calls during auto-bundle (~line 853-927), for each product about to be reserved, check if its reserved value exceeds the count of active appointments using that product. If orphaned reservations detected, reset reserved to the legitimate count before proceeding with the new reservation. This catches stranded reservations at the exact moment they'd cause "EXHAUSTED" false positives — when the next vet tries to use that product. Both layers are idempotent and safe to run concurrently. | P2 | 1-1.5 hrs | T4.169 | TODO | Crash recovery — stranded reservations block stock indefinitely without Cloud Functions. The Inventory page reconcile catches drift on staff inspection; the CW piggyback catches it at the point of clinical impact. |

| T4.172 | Remove multi-pet booking/visit support — simplify to single-pet-per-appointment across entire ecosystem — The multi-pet system (visitGroupId, groupSize, groupIndex, petServiceMap, selectedPets, siblingAppointments, groupAppointments) spans 17 files with 250 references and 5 known coordination bugs (stagger time not displayed, no pet-presence check at group check-in, blind group EOD resolution, partial group cancel orphans visitGroupId, sign-off guard doesn't transition siblings). The complexity exceeds what a solo developer can maintain and defend. Replace with single-pet-per-booking throughout. MOBILE (5 files, Day 1 ~4 hrs): (1) BookAppointment.js (67 refs): flatten selectedPets to single pet selection, remove petServiceMap (use flat selectedServices), remove stagger/cumulative capacity logic, simplify price summary to one pet, remove visitGroupId/groupSize/groupIndex from transaction payload, remove "Apply to All" UI, remove multi-pet step 2 per-pet service pickers. (2) useBookingEngine.js (8 refs): remove parallel department scheduling across pets (keep within-pet parallel), remove virtual bookings accumulator, remove stagger offset — slot duration = single pet's max parallel dept duration. (3) ClientAppointments.js (14 refs): remove group cancel logic, remove GROUP-BOOKING chips, remove group-level actions — each appointment is independent. (4) QueueScreen.js (10 refs): remove group position display, remove multi-pet queue awareness — show single pet's position only. (5) SelfCheckInScreen.js (5 refs): remove batch arrive for siblings — check in one pet at a time. ADMIN (12 files, Day 2 ~4 hrs): (6) Queue.jsx (33 refs): remove visitGroupId clustering/annotation logic (isGroupHeader/Mid/Tail, _visitGroupSize, _visitGroupIndex), simplify row processing to flat list, remove sibling discovery for AssignStaffModal/ClinicalWorkspace props. (7) queueColumns.jsx (12 refs): remove GROUP 1/2 chips, remove shared ticket group display, remove group header/tail conditional styling. (8) AssignStaffModal.jsx (16 refs): remove siblingAppointments prop, remove group check-in toggle, always single-pet check-in — modal becomes a simple confirmation (already simplified in T3.120). (9) ClinicalWorkspace.jsx (12 refs): remove groupAppointments prop, remove pet switching arrows (onSwitchPatient), remove sign-off sibling guard. (10) POSModal.jsx (17 refs): remove group billing branch — always individual checkout, no multi-appointment batching. (11) EndOfDayModal.jsx (14 refs): remove group clustering in silos, remove group-level resolution card with per-pet override — each appointment resolves independently. (12) Records.jsx (14 refs): remove group chip display, remove sibling panel in audit popover. (13) useQueueActions.js (4 refs): remove visitGroupId from carry-over clone and quickAdmitER. (14) sendPushNotification.js (5 refs): remove visitGroupId from notification payload. (15) pulseEventBuilders.js + pulseEventWriters.test.js (8 refs): remove GROUP-BOOKING chip from test builders. SETTINGS (1 file): (16) Settings.jsx (7 refs): remove maxPetsPerBooking field from defaults, parse, validation, save payload, tracked fields, and the UI TextField. (17) useClinicSettings.js: remove maxPetsPerBooking from DEFAULT_SETTINGS if present. APPROACH: Do NOT delete visitGroupId/groupSize/groupIndex from existing Firestore appointment documents — they're historical data. Just stop writing them on new appointments and stop reading them for UI grouping. All conditional branches like `if (visitGroupId) { ... }` become dead code that can be deleted. The `else` branch (single-pet path) is already the correct behavior. Existing multi-pet appointments from before this change will render as individual appointments — no migration needed. THESIS FRAMING: Mention multi-pet as "designed and prototyped" with data model in place, scoped to single-pet for quality. The visitGroupId field design and parallel scheduling algorithm can be cited as architectural investments. | P2 | 6-8 hrs | T4.171 | DONE | Day 1: mobile 5 files — useBookingEngine single-pet signature, BookAppointment flat state + single transaction, ClientAppointments group cancel deleted, QueueScreen flat tickets, SelfCheckInScreen individual queue numbers. Day 2: admin 12 files — Queue.jsx clustering removed, queueColumns GROUP chips deleted, AssignStaffModal single-pet only, ClinicalWorkspace pet-switching removed, POSModal group billing deleted (retail kept), EndOfDayModal flat resolution, Records.jsx group panel removed, useQueueActions/sendPushNotification/WalkInModal visitGroupId removed, test fixtures cleaned, Settings maxPetsPerBooking removed. 0 visitGroupId refs in entire codebase. Bundle -24kB. 322/322 tests pass. |

| T4.178 | QueueScreen redesign — personal ticket hero, per-department queue breakdown, multi-lane "Now Serving", two-row breadcrumb, stage-aware messaging, full transparency — QueueScreen.js currently leads with someone else's "Now Serving" number in a 240px circle, truncates all breadcrumb labels, shows only one department's queue position, has no pet name/services/vet on the personal ticket, shows "consultation room" regardless of status, displays "~0 mins" indefinitely when countdown expires, reads daily_queue.currentServing as a single-lane FIFO display for what is actually a D/M/c concurrent department queue, and has 5 hardcoded hex colors. Multi-pet section (lines 612-676) is dead code after T4.172. Redesign: LAYOUT HIERARCHY: (1) Delete redundant "Clinic Queue Monitor" header — nav header "LIVE QUEUE" is sufficient. (2) Personal ticket becomes the HERO — move above "Now Serving", large ticket number with pet name + full services[] list as subtitle ("Luluw · Anti tick & flea, Consultation") + assigned vet ("Dr. Santos") when not Unassigned. (3) "Now Serving" display — replace single-lane daily_queue.currentServing circle with per-department "now serving" filtered to the pet owner's relevant departments. Read active-status appointments (in-consult, dispensing, billing, on-hold) from the existing lobby listener, group by serviceCategory. Show one "now serving" row per department the pet's services belong to: "NOW SERVING IN GENERAL: A-003" / "NOW SERVING IN GROOMING: A-001 (2 ahead of you)". Single-department bookings show one row. Multi-department bookings show multiple rows so the pet owner sees where each of their services stands. When the clinic operates as single-department (strict FIFO), only one row renders — same architecture supports both models. Square neubrutalist box, borderRadius 0, not a circle. QUEUE POSITION: (4) Multi-department breakdown — replace single serviceCategory filter with per-department calculation. Read myTicket.services[] to get all unique departments. For each department, count ahead + estimate wait independently. Headline shows bottleneck (longest wait): "Estimated wait: ~75 min". Breakdown section shows all departments: "General: 5 ahead · ~75 min (bottleneck) / Grooming: 2 ahead · ~40 min". Uses existing lobbyPatients serviceCategory field — zero new queries. Modifies the useMemo at lines 243-283. (5) Elapsed wait counter — show "Waiting for: 30 min" from myTicket.timeArrived alongside "Estimated remaining: ~15 min". Live counter using setInterval 60s. When elapsed exceeds estimate: "Waiting for: 45 min — running ~10 min over estimate". BREADCRUMB: (6) Two-row wrap — 3 stages per row, full text visible. Row 1: Confirmed · Checked in · With the vet. Row 2: Pharmacy · Checkout · Done. Uses flexWrap:'wrap' with fixed ~33% width per stage. Current stage highlighted Sky Blue, past stages green, future gray. On-hold/confined sub-annotation stays. MESSAGING: (7) Stage-aware "IT'S YOUR TURN" text — arrived/confirmed→"Please proceed to the consultation room", dispensing→"Your medications are being prepared at the pharmacy", billing→"Please proceed to the cashier for checkout", confined→"Your pet is being monitored. Contact the clinic for updates.", on-hold→"Consultation paused — your pet is resting." (8) Countdown 0 handling — when countdown reaches 0 and still waiting, replace "~0 mins" with "Almost there — running slightly behind." CLEANUP: (9) Delete multi-pet section (lines 612-676) — visitGroupId grouping, visit group wrappers, per-pet status rows. Dead code after T4.172. (10) Replace hardcoded colors — #FFF3E0→COLORS.kpiOrangeBg, #FFB74D→COLORS.kpiOrangeBorder, #EFEBE9→COLORS.panelBg, #F0F9FF→COLORS.kpiBlueBg, rgba(255,255,255,0.8)→COLORS.white with opacity. EMPTY STATE: (11) "Book Now" button on "You are not in the queue" state — navigates to BookAppointment. Currently a dead end with no action. | T4.179 | Lobby Monitor redesign — multi-lane D/M/c display, per-department "Now Serving", estimated wait on upcoming, clock, queue depth — Monitor.jsx currently reads daily_queue.currentServing as a single-lane FIFO display, but VetConnect operates a D/M/c concurrent department queue. When Grooming serves A-001 and General serves A-003 simultaneously, the Monitor only shows whichever was last updated. The 240px display card takes 65% of a 1080p TV for one ticket. No clock, no queue depth, no estimated wait on upcoming tickets, no pet names, hardcoded "Please proceed to Consultation Room" regardless of status. Redesign: DATA SOURCE: (1) Replace single-ticket fetchTicketDetails (getDocs by queueNumber) with onSnapshot listener on active-status appointments: where status in ['in-consult', 'dispensing', 'billing', 'on-hold'], where scheduledDate >= startOfDay. Group results by serviceCategory. Each active appointment IS a "now serving" in its department. Delete fetchSeqRef race-condition logic (no longer needed — onSnapshot replaces one-shot fetch). Keep daily_queue listener for pause/active state only. MULTI-LANE DISPLAY: (2) Replace single Card with flex row of per-department lane cards. Each lane: department name + color (from existing getDeptColor), ticket number (formatted), pet name (petName field on appointment), full services[] list, ticket type chip (EMERGENCY/WALK-IN/APPOINTMENT). Single-department clinic: one card, full width (strict FIFO appearance). Multi-department clinic: 2-3 cards side by side (D/M/c). Same code handles both — loop over departments, render one card per active department. (3) Empty department lanes don't render — only departments currently serving a patient appear. UPCOMING PREVIEW: (4) Add estimated wait per upcoming ticket — use per-department avg consult duration (same calculation as QueueScreen T4.178). Show "UP NEXT: A-007 (~15 min)" instead of just "UP NEXT: A-007". Upcoming section already grouped by department from T4.134 — just add the time estimate. (5) Add confirmed patients as "EXPECTED" section below upcoming — query status='confirmed' for today, show as muted cards: "EXPECTED: A-010 — 3:00 PM". Gives lobby a sense of upcoming demand. CONTEXT: (6) Clock — current time displayed in the corner, auto-updating every minute via setInterval. "2:45 PM" in large readable font. Pet owners glance at this constantly. (7) Queue depth — "5 patients waiting" computed from lobby listener (arrived status count). Shown below the lane cards. (8) Enhanced footer — clinic name (from clinicSettings, already exists) + operating hours ("Open until 5:00 PM" from clinicSettings.closeHour) + clinic phone (from clinicSettings.clinicPhone). (9) After-hours display — when current hour >= closeHour or < openHour, show "Clinic closed. Opens tomorrow at {openHour}:00 AM" instead of "Waiting for next patient". Uses clinicSettings.openHour/closeHour. CLEANUP: (10) Replace "Please proceed to Consultation Room" (hardcoded line 281) — remove entirely. The Monitor shows who's being served, not where to go. Pet owners get directions from reception. (11) Square off all elements — the main display already uses borderRadius 0 + offset shadow. Ensure upcoming cards and lane cards match. No circles. (12) Delete fetchTicketDetails function + fetchSeqRef (replaced by onSnapshot). (13) Keep getDeptColor utility + departments one-shot fetch (already good). Decisions locked: multi-lane via active-status onSnapshot (not daily_queue.currentServing), no stage-aware directions (removed from scope), after-hours display, clock + depth + enhanced footer. Same architecture supports FIFO (1 lane) and multi-dept (N lanes). Single file: VetConnect-Admin/src/pages/Monitor.jsx. | P2 | 2-2.5 hrs | T4.172 | DONE | fetchTicketDetails replaced with active-status onSnapshot grouped by serviceCategory. Multi-lane per-dept Paper cards (flex layout, borderRadius:0, dept-color accent). Upcoming with ~N min estimates + EXPECTED confirmed section. Auto-updating clock (setInterval 60s). Queue depth from arrived count. Enhanced footer (closeHour + clinicPhone). After-hours display (closed message). "Please proceed to Consultation Room" deleted. isQueueIdle from activeServing. Also propagate petAllergies to active appointments for this pet (same pattern as EditPetModal lines 73-85). | T4.182 | Dashboard complete redesign — 4-tab layout (TODAY/ANALYTICS/FINANCIAL/PERFORMANCE), drag-drop removal, lab+vaccine+diagnosis statistics, visualization upgrades, Forensic Reports merge, financial depth, operational intelligence, conversion funnel — The dashboard currently has 4 tabs (Growth/Operations/Clinical/Financial) with blurry boundaries, drag-and-drop layout (complexity for zero value), quick nav tiles duplicating sidebar, full-width charts for 1-3 data points, stacked bars for 2-value comparisons, no lab/vaccine/diagnosis statistics despite catalog data, a separate "Forensic Reports" page that should be integrated, and missing metrics across all tabs. (2) Delete QuickNavTiles.jsx. (3) Delete GrowthTab.jsx + ClinicalTab.jsx (content merges into AnalyticsTab). (4) Delete standalone Reports.jsx page + /reports route + Sidebar "Forensic Reports" entry + DateRangePicker.jsx. Absorb ConsultPerformanceTab + AuditIntegrityTab + StaffWorkloadTab content into new PERFORMANCE dashboard tab. Rename all "Forensic" references. TAB RESTRUCTURE — 4 tabs: (5) TODAY (was Operations, always "today"): existing 10 KPIs in 3 grouped rows + alert strip + reminders + dept load + staff workload. NEW: estimated queue clear time ("Queue clears by ~4:30 PM" — avg consult × remaining ÷ active staff), remaining appointments today ("5 more expected: 3 confirmed, 2 pending" — pending+confirmed for today, not already completed), patient flow rate ("2.5 patients/hour" — completed ÷ hours since clinic open). (6) ANALYTICS (merges Growth + Clinical + new stats, period-selectable): PATIENTS section — New Clients KPI with sparkline (replace standalone bar chart), Total Clients, Total Pets, NEW New Pets Registered KPI (pets created in period — growth metric alongside new clients), species as inline text (replace stacked bar), Top Breeds list, Retention, Lead Time. APPOINTMENTS section — Total Appointments KPI with sparkline, Walk-in/Scheduled as side-by-side percentages (replace stacked bar), Utilization, Volume Trend bar with avg line, Peak Hours heatmap, Service Popularity horizontal bar, NEW No-Show Rate by Day of Week (group no-shows by weekday — "Mondays: 15%, Fridays: 3%" — horizontal bar or table, actionable for scheduling/overbooking), NEW Cancellation Reason Breakdown (aggregate auditReasons — "Client cancelled: 5, Clinic cancelled: 2" — horizontal bar or chips). CLINICAL section — Records Signed, Follow-up Compliance, Confinement Rate, NEW Amendment Rate ("8% of records amended" — count CLINICAL_AMENDMENT pulse events ÷ records signed — quality indicator). Top Diagnoses horizontal bar grouped by catalog category. NEW Diagnosis Category donut chart. NEW Severity Distribution stacked bar. NEW Diagnoses by Species. Vaccinations KPI + NEW Compliance Rate + NEW Overdue Count + Vaccines by Type horizontal bar. NEW Lab Tests Ordered KPI + NEW Normal/Abnormal/Critical segmented bar + NEW Most Ordered Tests horizontal bar + NEW Tests by Category donut + NEW Abnormal Rate by Test horizontal bar. Top Prescribed Items split by productClass. Records per Vet. Vitals by Species table. (7) FINANCIAL (period-selectable): existing 9 KPIs + revenue trend as line chart (replace bar) + revenue vs expenses overlay + payment methods + revenue by dept + expense categories. NEW: Collection Rate KPI (totalCollected / totalBilled as % — "92% collection rate"), Custom Discount Breakdown (split SC/PWD discounts from custom discounts — two KPIs instead of one combined), Revenue per Service Type (horizontal bar — "Consultation ₱45K, Grooming ₱12K" — more granular than by department), Revenue Forecast from Confirmed Bookings ("₱15,000 projected from 8 confirmed appointments" — query pending+confirmed × servicePrice — shows incoming revenue before it arrives), Deposit vs Collected Breakdown ("₱5K deposits, ₱45K checkouts"), Retail vs Clinical Revenue split (from T4.184 saleType field). (8) PERFORMANCE (absorbs Forensic Reports page): Consult Performance section — avg/median/P90 consult duration, distribution buckets, by-vet/dept/service breakdowns, queue-to-completion, transition matrix. Uses useForensicReportData hook triggered by Dashboard period selection (replaces manual "Generate" button). Audit Integrity section — pulse coverage, seal coverage, corrections, terminal reversals, event type counts. Staff Workload section — active vets, total hours, per-vet breakdown. NEW: Appointment Conversion Funnel (pending→confirmed→arrived→completed — what % drops at each stage — "80% confirmed, 90% arrived, 95% completed" — funnel or stepped bar visualization — thesis differentiator showing pipeline health). NEW: Staff Utilization Rate per vet ("Dr. Santos: 75% — 6h consult / 8h available" — consult hours ÷ clinic operating hours — more meaningful than raw counts). Day 2 (~5 hrs) = add all new statistics to useDashboardData (lab, vaccine, diagnosis, financial depth, TODAY intelligence, PERFORMANCE additions) + visualization replacements (sparklines, donuts, line chart, heatmap). | T4.183 | Visit Log redesign — rename from "Records", 3-tab restructure (Pending/Active/Completed), per-tab KPIs, date grouping, audit discoverability, case view headers — The Records page (Records.jsx) serves as the clinic's visit logbook but has organizational gaps: 6 tabs with blurry boundaries (ALL VISITS redundant with search, CLINICAL + IN-PATIENT both read-only, jargon names), audit popover hidden behind a tiny clock icon, no per-tab metrics, flat DataGrid with no date anchoring, cryptic action icons, barely differentiated Cases view. Redesign with 3 locked decisions + 5 active fixes: DECISIONS LOCKED: (1) Tab restructure — 6 tabs → 3: PENDING (pending + confirmed, absorbs TRIAGE — bulk reschedule + reassign + defer + void actions stay), ACTIVE (arrived + in-consult + on-hold + dispensing + billing + confined — merges CLINICAL + IN-PATIENT into one read-only tab), COMPLETED (completed + carried-over + cancelled + no-show — merges ARCHIVE + VOIDED). Remove ALL VISITS tab entirely — search bar + saved presets handle cross-tab lookups. Update SILO_MAP from 6 entries to 3. Update useGlobalRecords.js silo definitions. Update tab rendering in Records.jsx. Checkbox selection stays on PENDING tab only. (2) Rename page — "RECORDS" → "VISIT LOG" across: page title, sidebar menu item in Sidebar.jsx, route label if any, page header typography. (3) ACTIVE tab merges Clinical + In-Patient — one tab for all in-clinic statuses. Staff can browse active visits without going to Queue. Confined visits appear alongside in-consult/dispensing/billing — no separate In-Patient tab. ACTIVE FIXES: (4) Audit popover discoverability — make the entire DataGrid row clickable to open the audit popover (currently only the tiny TimelineIcon button opens it). Remove the standalone TimelineIcon from the actions column. Row click = audit. Fallback: if row click conflicts with checkbox selection on PENDING tab, keep icon on PENDING but make rows clickable on ACTIVE + COMPLETED tabs. (5) Per-tab KPI strip — add a compact horizontal KPI row between the tab bar and the DataGrid showing status breakdown for the active tab. PENDING: "{N} pending · {N} confirmed". ACTIVE: "{N} arrived · {N} in-consult · {N} dispensing · {N} billing · {N} confined · {N} on-hold". COMPLETED: "{N} completed · {N} carried-over · {N} cancelled · {N} no-show". Computed from the loaded records array, not a separate query. Compact chips or inline text, not full KPI cards — one row, not a grid. (6) Case view summary headers — when viewMode === 'case', add a case header row above each chain group: "CASE: {petName} · {serviceName} · Day 1–{N} · {startDate} – {endDate}". Bolder visual separation: 2px accent top border, cream background, bold uppercase text. The current gray-background continuation styling stays for individual rows within a group. (7) Date section headers — insert visual date separators between rows when the date changes: "MONDAY, MAY 5" / "SUNDAY, MAY 4". Rendered as a non-interactive row spanning all columns with accent text on cream background. Same pattern as mobile My Bookings SectionList date grouping. Only in Visits mode (not Cases mode — cases have their own grouping). (8) Action column cleanup — reduce from 5 cryptic icons to 3 with better discoverability: "Audit" as a visible text button (replaces TimelineIcon — but only on PENDING tab where row click is reserved for checkbox), CRM link icon (PersonIcon, keep with clearer tooltip: "View in Patient CRM"), Print icon (keep). Remove Copy ID button (developer tool, not clinical — staff never uses raw appointment IDs). Remove the standalone TimelineIcon on ACTIVE + COMPLETED tabs (replaced by row-click from fix #4). CLEANUP: (9) Remove visitGroupId GROUP badges and "N/M" position chips from identity column — dead UI after T4.172. (10) Remove "Visit Group Siblings" section from audit popover — dead after T4.172. | T4.184 | Standalone retail POS — non-appointment product sales from 3 entry points — The POS is currently appointment-gated: the only path to POSModal is Queue → billing status → "OPEN CHECKOUT." PH vet clinics sell retail products (food, treats, accessories, grooming supplies) alongside clinical services. A customer buying dog food with no pet appointment has no way to check out — staff must create a fake walk-in, skip consult, advance to billing, then open POS. 5-step workaround for a 1-step sale. Fix: add a "standalone" mode to POSModal where patient prop is null + 3 entry points + retail sale doc schema. STANDALONE MODE (POSModal.jsx): (1) When opened without a patient prop (patient === null), POSModal renders in retail mode: empty cart (no encounterItems from consult), product search + add from inventory only (no services — Decision 3: A), customer identification optional with soft nudge before checkout ("Link to a client for purchase history?" with client search Autocomplete + "Skip — Counter Sale" button — Decision 1: C). All existing features work: cash change (T4.148), custom discounts (T4.149), split-tender (T4.150), SC/PWD discount (T2.105), receipt PDF + email (T4.152), sequential receipt numbering (T4.153), FIFO batch inventory deduction. No status transition (no appointment to complete). No encounterItems pre-load. (2) Sale doc schema: add saleType field — 'retail' for standalone, 'clinical' for appointment-based (backward compat: existing sales without saleType treated as 'clinical'). Retail sales write: saleType:'retail', appointmentId:null, ownerId (from linked client or null for anonymous), ownerName (from linked client or 'Counter Sale'), petName:null, petId:null. All other fields identical to clinical sales (items, total, discount, paymentMethod, paymentTenders, receiptNumber, etc.). Decision 2: A — explicit saleType field. (3) If customer linked: write ownerId + ownerName to sale doc. If anonymous: ownerId:null, ownerName:'Counter Sale'. Nudge dialog appears after cart is filled, before payment — MUI Dialog with client search Autocomplete + "LINK CLIENT" button + "SKIP — COUNTER SALE" outlined button. Linked sales appear in client purchase history (PatientDashboard billing ledger, ClientHeader outstanding balance). Anonymous sales appear in Transactions page but not in any client's history. ENTRY POINTS (3 locations): (4) Queue page header — "RETAIL SALE" button next to "ADD WALK-IN". Sky Blue outlined, ShoppingCartIcon. onClick opens POSModal with patient={null}. Staff's home base — natural location next to walk-in registration. (5) Transactions page header — "NEW SALE" button next to existing header actions. Same styling. onClick opens POSModal with patient={null}. Logical for staff reviewing sales who realize a sale was missed. (6) Sidebar — "Quick Sale" menu item below Transactions. ShoppingCartIcon. onClick opens POSModal with patient={null} via a global state or context. Accessible from any page without navigation. Alternatively: a persistent FAB (floating action button) in the bottom-right corner across all pages instead of a sidebar item — more discoverable but more intrusive. Decision: sidebar menu item (less intrusive, consistent with existing nav pattern). DASHBOARD INTEGRATION: (7) useDashboardData.js Financial block: add retailRevenue (sum where saleType==='retail') + clinicalRevenue (sum where saleType!=='retail'). FinancialTab/AnalyticsTab: add "Retail vs Clinical Revenue" horizontal bar or two KPI cards showing the split. (8) Transactions page (Sales.jsx): add a "Type" filter chip or column showing RETAIL / CLINICAL badge per sale. Filter: All / Retail Only / Clinical Only. FIRESTORE RULES: (9) No changes needed — sales collection already allows isAuth() create. The sale doc just has different field values, not a different collection. Decisions locked: C (customer optional with nudge), A (saleType field: 'retail' vs 'clinical'), A (inventory products only, no services). Files: POSModal.jsx (standalone mode guard: if !patient, retail UI — no services section, no appointment status transition, nudge dialog), Queue.jsx (RETAIL SALE button), Sales.jsx (NEW SALE button + type filter/column), Sidebar.jsx (Quick Sale menu item), useDashboardData.js (retail/clinical revenue split), FinancialTab or AnalyticsTab (revenue split visualization). | P2 | 3-4 hrs | T4.142 | DONE | Day 1: POSModal standalone mode (isRetailMode gate, handleRetailCheckout separate transaction, customer nudge dialog, generateRetailReceiptHTML, saleType backfill). Queue RETAIL SALE button. Day 2: Sales.jsx inventory listeners + joinedInventory + NEW SALE button + POSModal render + auto-open from location.state + saleType filter (All/Retail/Clinical) + saleType column (RETAIL/CLINICAL chips). Sidebar Quick Sale menu item → navigate with state. useDashboardData retailRevenue/clinicalRevenue/retailTransactionCount. Build clean, 322/322 tests pass. |
| T4.185 | EMRDrawer redesign — search, filters, date headers, CW styling parity, responsive width, Queue access — EMRDrawer.jsx (520px fixed-width right Drawer, z-index 1400) shows a pet's full medical record history as collapsible cards during ClinicalWorkspace consults. Currently missing: search, filters, timeline/date anchoring, record count, print/export, active record highlight, and accessible only from ClinicalWorkspace. Styling uses its own SectionBlock/SoapText components instead of matching ClinicalWorkspace patterns. Redesign: SEARCH + FILTER (1) Add search TextField at the top of the drawer (below pet name header) — filters records by keyword across diagnosis, subjective, plan, vet name, prescription names. Client-side filter against loaded records array. Same search pattern as PetHistoryScreen (T3.94). (2) Add compact filter chips below search: All / Medical / Grooming / Vaccination (filter by serviceType or department). Date range filter: "Last 6 months" / "Last year" / "All time" chips. Record count display: "12 records" updated as filters narrow. DATE HEADERS (3) Insert month/year section headers between records when the month changes: "MAY 2026" / "APRIL 2026". Rendered as non-interactive Typography with cream background + accent text + uppercase. Cards stay as flat collapsible list below each header. Decision 1: B — date section headers (no dot timeline, drawer too narrow). RESPONSIVE WIDTH (4) Replace fixed width: 520 with responsive: width: '35vw', minWidth: 400, maxWidth: 650. 35% of 1920px = 672px (spacious for SOAP reading), 35% of 1366px = 478px (compact but usable). Decision 2: B — responsive percentage. STYLING PARITY (5) Match ClinicalWorkspace visual language: section labels use FONT + TYPE.label pattern, SOAP quadrant colors (S blue, O neutral, A green, P accent) match SoapGrid rendering, vitals use the same VitalsGrid compact layout, diagnosis chips match SoapGrid severity styling, discharge section uses cream background (same as CW discharge rendering). Replace standalone SectionBlock/SoapText sub-components with shared patterns or align their styling to CW. ACTIVE RECORD HIGHLIGHT (6) When the drawer is open during an active consult, highlight the current visit's record card (match by appointmentId). Show a "CURRENT VISIT" badge on the card header. If the record hasn't been signed yet, show draft preview: "Draft in progress — not yet signed" with muted styling. Differentiates today's work from historical records. PRINT/EXPORT (7) Add a "Print Full EMR" button in the drawer header. Reuses printVisitSummary.js utility but iterates all loaded records into one HTML document. Grouped by date with page breaks between years. Opens in print window same as existing print pattern. QUEUE ACCESS (8) Add "View Pet History" MenuItem in Queue.jsx overflow menu (the MoreVert ⋮ menu per row, alongside existing items like Put On Hold, Resume Consult, etc.). onClick opens EMRDrawer with petId from the row's appointment doc. Requires EMRDrawer to accept petId as a prop and fetch medical_records independently (currently it receives records as a prop from ClinicalWorkspace). Refactor: EMRDrawer accepts either records[] prop (CW path, already loaded) OR petId prop (Queue path, fetches on mount via getDocs query on medical_records where petId == prop). Queue.jsx adds EMRDrawer render + state: emrPetId + emrOpen. The overflow MenuItem sets emrPetId and opens the drawer. Files: EMRDrawer.jsx (search, filters, date headers, responsive width, styling, active highlight, print, petId fetch mode), Queue.jsx (overflow MenuItem + EMRDrawer render + state), queueColumns.jsx (MenuItem addition in overflow menu). Decisions locked: B (date section headers, no dot timeline), B (responsive 35vw, min 400, max 650). | P2 | 3-4 hrs | — | DONE | Full rewrite: search TextField (keyword across diagnosis/subjective/plan/vet/Rx), filter chips (All/Medical/Grooming/Vaccination + 6mo/1yr/All time) + record count badge, month/year date section headers (cream), responsive width (35vw min:400 max:650), TYPE.label styling parity (SectionBlock/SoapText deleted), collapsible Objective with examSummaryLine, 7-vital grid with "not taken", Assessment hero, cream discharge, active record highlight (Sky Blue border + CURRENT VISIT chip + draft notice), Print Full EMR button (page breaks by year), Queue access (history Dialog replaced with EMRDrawer + petId fetch + Snackbar walk-in guard), CW appointmentId prop. 13/13 spec items verified. |
| T4.186 | My Stats screen — dedicated pet owner analytics with per-pet health cards, preventive care timeline, spending breakdown, relationship metrics — The ClientDashboard stats section (T4.156: 9 KPI cards, visit chart, weight trends, vaccine alert, age milestones) is buried 2-3 scrolls below quick actions. Most pet owners never discover it. All metrics are aggregate — "3 pets, 67% compliant, ₱16,450 spent" with no per-pet granularity, no medication tracking, no recheck countdowns, no spending per pet, no actionable booking CTAs. Move to a dedicated "My Stats" screen accessible via quick action tile on ClientDashboard. SCREEN STRUCTURE: (1) Register MyStatsScreen in App.js navigation stack. Add "📊 MY STATS" quick action tile on ClientDashboard grid (same pattern as My Bookings, Live Queue, etc.). Remove the entire statsSection from ClientDashboard.js (lines ~1091-1204) — dashboard becomes purely action-oriented (balance banner + SuperCard + quick actions + reminders). (2) YOUR RELATIONSHIP section — client since date (user.createdAt), total visits lifetime, average spend per visit, visit frequency ("every 6 weeks"), follow-up compliance ("2/3 attended, 67%"), no-show count, profile completeness percentage (check user doc fields: address, city, email, emergencyContacts, govIdType — show "80% complete — add Government ID" nudge with link to UserProfileScreen), consent status (DPA ✅ Signed v1 / ⚠ Action needed + Waiver ✅ / ⚠). All computed from existing user doc + appointments array — zero new queries. (3) VISIT TRENDS section — move the 6-month visit bar chart from ClientDashboard. Keep as-is with neubrutalism styling. (4) YOUR PETS section — per-pet health cards, one card per pet. Each card shows: pet name + species emoji + age + current weight with delta + weight sparkline (last 5 readings from medical_records vitals — one getDocs per pet on mount), last visit date + service type (from appointments grouped by petId), vaccine status per pet with countdown (current ✅ / due soon ⚠ with "in N days" / overdue 🔴 with "N days overdue" — from vaccineAdministrations + vaccine catalog, same computation as compliance %), active medications (dispensedProducts from medical_records in last 90 days where isDrug — one getDocs per pet, show name + "5 days remaining" if end date computable from instructions), allergy list from pet doc petAllergies, next recheck due with countdown (from most recent medical_record dischargeSummary.recheckIn — compute due date from record date + interval), "BOOK NOW" button on overdue vaccines, "BOOK RECHECK" button on due rechecks — both navigate to BookAppointment with prefilled petId + serviceType. Active conditions from problems sub-collection (T4.13 dependency — show "Active: CKD Stage II, Dental Grade 2" if problems exist, graceful empty state if T4.13 not built). (5) SPENDING section — monthly spending trend sparkline (last 6 months, from sales grouped by month), spending per pet (from sales items grouped by petId — "Luluw ₱8,500 · Chen ₱3,200"), spending by service type (from sales items grouped by serviceType — "Consultation ₱9,000 · Grooming ₱4,500"), outstanding balance summary ("₱0 — all clear" or "₱200 outstanding from May 4 visit"). DIAGNOSIS HISTORY section (between YOUR PETS and SPENDING): (7) Per-pet diagnosis additions to the health cards — add "Conditions: 2 active, 3 resolved" count line + "Latest: Ear Infection (May 4)" last diagnosis with date. Data from pets/{petId}/problems sub-collection (T4.13) for active/resolved counts + most recent medical_records.diagnoses[] for latest diagnosis. Graceful fallback: if T4.13 not built, show diagnosis history from medical_records only (no active/resolved distinction — flat list). (8) NEW DIAGNOSIS HISTORY section — rendered between YOUR PETS and SPENDING sections. Contains: Total Conditions KPI (lifetime count across all pets — "8 conditions diagnosed"), Active vs Resolved inline text ("3 active · 5 resolved"), Per-pet diagnosis timeline (each pet's diagnoses listed chronologically — "Luluw: CKD Stage II (active since Apr 2026) · Dental Grade 2 (resolved May 2026)" — active in red/orange accent, resolved in green with checkmark), Most Recurring Diagnosis ("Ear Infection: 3 occurrences across 2 pets" — highlights patterns the owner should discuss with the vet), Resolved This Year count with positive framing ("4 conditions resolved this year"). Data sources: pets/{petId}/problems sub-collection for active/resolved status + severity + dates (one getDocs per pet on mount — same query as the per-pet health card conditions), medical_records.diagnoses[] for historical occurrence counting (already loaded for per-pet medication queries). If T4.13 (problem list) is not built: fall back to aggregating diagnoses[] from medical_records across all visits per pet — show as flat chronological list without active/resolved distinction, no severity tracking, label section as "DIAGNOSIS HISTORY" instead of "CONDITIONS". The section degrades gracefully. (6) PREVENTIVE CARE section — consolidated timeline of upcoming + overdue care items sorted by urgency: overdue vaccines (🔴 with BOOK NOW), upcoming vaccines within 30 days (⚠ with countdown), recheck reminders (🔬 with countdown + BOOK RECHECK), age milestones (🎂 moved from ClientDashboard — "Mooncake turns 7 in 2 months — senior screening recommended"). Each item has a booking CTA where applicable. DATA: new useMyStats.js hook (or extend existing useClientStats.js) that adds: per-pet medical_records queries (medications, vitals for sparkline, recheck dates), per-pet spending aggregation, profile completeness computation, preventive care timeline builder. The existing allAppointments + userPets + sales listeners from ClientDashboard can be passed as props or the hook can re-query independently. DESIGN: neubrutalism via mobileTokens.js (already established by T4.156 KPICard pattern). Per-pet cards use SHADOW.card offset shadow, borderRadius 0, 2px accent border. Section headers uppercase with letterSpacing. "BOOK NOW" buttons use COLORS.sky with press-snap. ScrollView with section spacing. Files: NEW MyStatsScreen.js, ClientDashboard.js (remove stats section + add quick action tile), App.js (register route), useClientStats.js or NEW useMyStats.js (per-pet queries + spending + diagnosis aggregation + preventive timeline). | P2 | 5-6 hrs | — | DONE | 2-day build.
