Day 2: DIAGNOSIS HISTORY (totalConditions, perPetTimeline, mostRecurring, thisYearCount), SPENDING BREAKDOWN (monthly sparkline, per-pet, per-service, outstanding balance), PREVENTIVE CARE (urgency-sorted: overdue vaccines BOOK NOW, due-soon, recheck BOOK RECHECK, milestones). | T4.193 | My Stats visual enrichment — charts, gauges, drill-downs, upcoming appointments, date range selector — MyStatsScreen is text-heavy with only 2 charts (visit bar + spending sparkline) and tiny weight sparklines. Other clinic client portals (PetDesk, Digitail, MyChart) are dramatically more visual. 12 enhancements across 3 tiers: TIER 1 — HIGH IMPACT (~1.5 hrs): (1) Spending bar chart — replace the monthly spending SparkLine with a proper bar chart (same pattern as visit trends). Show ₱ amount labels on each bar. Bars are more readable than lines for financial data. (2) Vaccination compliance gauge per pet — circular SVG progress ring on each pet health card showing "3/5 current" with green (>80%) / orange (50-80%) / red (<50%) fill. Data already exists in petCard.vaccineStatus.completeness. react-native-svg Arc component. (3) Visit type pie/donut chart — new section between VISIT TRENDS and YOUR PETS. Break down visits by department/serviceCategory (Consultation 45%, Grooming 30%, Vaccination 25%). Data from allAppointments grouped by serviceCategory. react-native-svg Pie with CHART_COLORS cycling. (4) Total lifetime spend KPI — add to YOUR RELATIONSHIP section or SPENDING section header. Sum all salesData totals: "₱47,350 total lifetime spend." One line of reduce(). TIER 2 — MEDIUM IMPACT (~2.5 hrs): (5) Year-over-year visit comparison — side-by-side bar groups: current year vs previous year, per month. Shows growth or decline pattern. New chart below VISIT TRENDS. Data from allAppointments grouped by year+month. (6) Date range selector on spending — toggle chips: This Year / Last Year / All Time (currently hardcoded to last 6 months). Filters the spending sparkline/bars + per-pet + per-service breakdowns. State: spendingRange. (7) Upcoming appointments section — NEW section between YOUR RELATIONSHIP and VISIT TRENDS. Shows pending + confirmed appointments with countdown ("Anti tick & flea — in 3 days"), pet name, scheduled date+time. Data from allAppointments where status in ['pending','confirmed'] and scheduledDate >= today. Empty state: "No upcoming appointments — book a visit!" with CTA. (8) Per-pet spending drill-down — tapping a pet's spending row expands to show individual transaction history for that pet (date, service, amount). Data from salesData matched to appointments by petId. Collapsible with Collapse animation. TIER 3 — NICE-TO-HAVE (~4 hrs): (9) Weight trend full chart — replace tiny 5-point sparkline with a tappable expanded chart showing ALL historical weight readings with breed-normal range band (if available from species vital ranges). Modal or inline expand with larger SparkLine (height:120 instead of 40). (10) Lab result trends — mini sparklines per lab test (CBC, glucose, etc.) on pet health cards. Data from medical_records.labResults[] grouped by testName, last 5 numeric results per test. Show only tests with 2+ readings. (11) Medication adherence tracking — per active medication: "Amoxicillin: 3/7 days" with mini progress bar. Computed from sig.days + prescription recordDate. Green when on track, orange when >50% elapsed, red when overdue. (12) Seasonal visit patterns — 12-month heatmap strip showing visit density per month across ALL years. Warmer color = more visits. Reveals patterns ("we always come in March and September"). Single row of 12 cells (Jan-Dec) with opacity-based shading. Decisions needed before implementation: None — all items are additive visualizations using existing data. No new Firestore queries needed (all data already loaded via route params from ClientDashboard). No design decisions — all use existing SparkLine/SVG patterns + mobileTokens styling. 3-day split recommended: Day 1 = Tier 1 (items 1-4, ~1.5 hrs) + item 7 upcoming appointments (~30 min). Day 2 = Tier 2 remainder (items 5-6-8, ~2 hrs). Day 3 = Tier 3 (items 9-12, ~4 hrs). | T4.194 | PetHistoryScreen tabbed restructure + vitals/vaccines enrichment — Replace 4 collapsible sections (snapshot, vitals, vaccination, medications) with a 4-tab layout (RECORDS default / VITALS / VACCINES / OVERVIEW). AI FAB stays floating across all tabs. Search bar + department filter + year/month picker above tabs, active only on RECORDS tab. TAB RESTRUCTURE (~2 hrs): (1) Add MUI-style tab bar below the search/filter row. 4 tabs: RECORDS (medical record FlatList + date headers + search), VITALS (vital sparklines fullscreen), VACCINES (vaccination status cards), OVERVIEW (snapshot + medications merged). Default to RECORDS tab. Tab state persists within the screen session. Department filter + year picker render conditionally (only on RECORDS tab). The record FlatList only mounts on RECORDS tab (performance — avoids rendering 50+ cards off-screen). VITALS TAB ENHANCEMENTS (~3 hrs): (2) All 7 vitals displayed equally — same height, same treatment, no prioritization. Each vital gets more vertical space than the current collapsible card (height:80 instead of 40). (3) Reference range bands on each sparkline — green zone overlay for species-normal values. SparkLine component already supports normalRange prop. Pass SPECIES_VITAL_RANGES from ClinicalWorkspace (or define locally) keyed by petSpecies. Canine: temp 37.5-39.2, HR 60-140bpm, RR 10-30rpm, CRT 1-2s, BCS 4-5. Feline: temp 38.0-39.5, HR 120-220bpm, RR 20-42rpm, CRT 1-2s, BCS 4-5. Weight + Pain have no universal range (breed-dependent / subjective). (4) Normal/abnormal label next to latest value — green "Normal" or red "Abnormal" based on whether the latest reading falls within the reference range. For vitals without ranges (weight, pain), show no label. (5) Tap-to-zoom on each vital — tapping a sparkline opens the existing VitalsZoomModal (already at line 888: vitalsZoom state) with full history, larger chart, reference band, all data points. Wire onPress from the trends section to setVitalsZoom. (6) Plain-language interpretation below each vital — "Temperature: 39C — Normal for felines" or "Heart Rate: 180 bpm — Within normal range" or "Weight: 10 kg — -4 kg since last visit". Uses species range + delta to generate a human-readable sentence. (7) Trend direction color on delta annotation — green arrow when value moves TOWARD normal range, red arrow when moving AWAY from normal. Not just "changed" but "changed in a good/bad direction." For vitals without ranges, use neutral color (current behavior). (8) Historical range strip below each sparkline — "Range: 8-14 kg across 5 visits" showing the min-max-count across all readings. One line of text. (9) Anomaly warning icon on tab badge — if ANY vital's latest reading is outside the species-normal range, show a small warning dot on the VITALS tab label: "VITALS" in danger color. Alerts pet owners without them needing to open the tab. VACCINES TAB ENHANCEMENTS (~2.5 hrs): (10) Sort vaccine cards by urgency — overdue first, then due-soon, then no-record, then current. Currently renders in catalog order. Sort the statuses array before rendering. (11) Hide "View history" toggle when history is empty — conditional render: only show when there are actual vaccination records for that vaccine. Currently shows an empty expandable for NO RECORD vaccines. (12) Recommended interval text per vaccine — convert intervalDays from vaccine catalog to human-readable: "Recommended every 1 year" / "Recommended every 3 years". One line below the vaccine name. (13) 0% empty state — when completeness is 0/N (no vaccinations at all), replace the empty progress bar with a message + CTA: "No vaccinations on record — protect your pet today" + BOOK VISIT button. The empty progress bar at 0% looks broken. (14) Per-vaccine BOOK VISIT button on overdue and no-record cards — not just the global BOOK VACCINATION VISIT at the bottom. Each card with status overdue or no-record gets its own "SCHEDULE" button navigating to BookAppointment with prefillPetId. (15) Upcoming vaccination schedule timeline — visual timeline showing next vaccination milestones: "Rabies → Now / FVRCP → 6 months / FeLV → 1 year." Sorted by due date. Shows the roadmap of upcoming vaccinations. (16) Core vs non-core labels per vaccine — "REQUIRED" (Rabies, RA 9482 Anti-Rabies Act), "CORE" (FVRCP/DHPP), "LIFESTYLE" (FeLV/Leptospirosis). Hardcoded for common PH vaccines, fallback to "RECOMMENDED" for unlabeled catalog entries. (2) Right-click contextual on calendar slots: right-clicking an appointment block or empty time slot opens the side panel with a pre-filled query containing the slot date/time + appointment details (pet name, owner, services, status) if clicking an existing appointment, or "What can I schedule at [time] on [date]?" if clicking an empty slot. (3) Quick action chips above the input field: "Tomorrow's Briefing" (summarize tomorrow), "Find a Slot" (capacity-aware slot search), "Staff Availability" (who's working when), "This Week Summary" (load overview), "Conflicts & Gaps" (detect overbooking or empty stretches). Chips + free-text input. (4) Data context — visible range + on-demand expansion: system prompt receives currently displayed week/month appointments formatted as structured summary + services catalog (names, durations, departments, prices) + staff list (names, departments, working days) + clinic settings (hours, slot intervals, closed dates) + department capacities + inventory stock levels (for vaccine/medication availability checks) + medical history summary for pets appearing in visible appointments (last diagnosis, allergies, last vitals — enables "what should I prepare for Niki's recheck?"). Dynamic appendix injected per query with formatted calendar data + staff + inventory + pet summaries. Prompt constrains responses to scheduling/operational topics — no clinical diagnosis, no treatment recommendations. (6) Read-only intelligence: AI answers questions and makes suggestions, staff takes action manually. No autonomous booking/cancellation/rescheduling. Future upgrade path to suggest+confirm pattern. (7) Multi-turn conversation: history maintained while panel is open, cleared on close. Same chatWithHistory pattern as ClinicalAIPanel. (8) No artificial rate limits: ~$0.005/query ($4-15/month at unlimited staff usage). No cooldown, no session cap. (9) Audit logging: all queries logged to llm_audit_logs with source:'calendar', staffId, query, response, timestamp. Same infrastructure as CW Ask AI. (10) Access: all authenticated staff (isAuth). No admin gating. (11) Settings integration: add Calendar AI section to Settings (Pillar alongside existing LLM config) with enable/disable toggle + system prompt editor + model selector (defaults to existing Worker config). (12) UI styling: matches Calendar page design language — cream card backgrounds, 2px espresso borders, neubrutalism borderRadius:0, FONT/TYPE/COLORS tokens from designTokens.js, consistent with Calendar popovers and appointment detail cards. | T4.204 | Balance reminder queue — pre-computed queue replacing direct sales query — The Worker handleBalanceReminders currently queries the sales collection directly via Firestore REST API, which returns 403 because sales requires isAuth() and the Worker is unauthenticated. Vaccine and appointment reminders work because they use pre-computed queue collections (vaccine_reminder_queue, appointment_reminder_queue) with public read rules. Fix: (1) Create balance_reminder_queue/{ownerId} collection with schema { ownerId, ownerName, ownerEmail, ownerPhone, pushToken, totalBalance, saleCount, lastSaleDate, updatedAt, lastReminderSentAt, balanceReminderSnoozedUntil }. (2) Firestore rules: allow read: if true; allow write: if isAuth(). (3) Piggyback write on POS checkout: when POSModal completes a sale with balanceRemaining > 0, upsert balance_reminder_queue/{ownerId} — sum all outstanding balances for that owner (query sales where ownerName matches and balanceRemaining > 0), write total + owner contact fields. When balanceRemaining === 0 (fully paid), re-check if owner has ANY other outstanding sales — if none, delete the queue doc. (4) Piggyback on "Mark as Settled" (useQueueActions or wherever settle happens): same re-check logic — recalculate total outstanding, update or delete queue doc. (5) Rewrite Worker handleBalanceReminders to read balance_reminder_queue instead of querying sales. Same pattern as handleVaccineReminders: iterate queue docs, check cooldown/snooze, send push + email, update lastReminderSentAt. Remove the sales structuredQuery entirely. (6) Move snooze/cooldown fields from user doc to queue doc (balanceReminderSnoozedUntil, lastBalanceReminderSentAt already on queue schema). (7) Optional: "Recompute Balance Queue" button in Settings alongside existing vaccine queue recompute — full sweep of sales collection, rebuilds all queue docs. For migration/recovery. Files: NEW balance_reminder_queue Firestore collection, firestore.rules (add rules), POSModal or usePOSCheckout (piggyback write), useQueueActions or settle handler (piggyback on settle), Worker handleBalanceReminders (rewrite to read queue), Settings.jsx (optional recompute button). | P2 | 1.5 hrs | T4.147 | DONE | Fixes the 403 error on Worker balance reminders. Same pre-computed queue architecture as vaccine + appointment reminders. Piggyback write eliminates manual step. |
| T4.205 | Booking TOCTOU race fix — slot reservation document pattern — The JIT capacity check in BookAppointment.js (getDocs query for overlapping appointments) runs OUTSIDE the runTransaction. Two clients booking the same department slot simultaneously both pass the pre-flight check and both succeed — the transaction only ensures atomicity of the individual write, not mutual exclusion. T2.87 upgraded writeBatch→runTransaction but didn't close the read-then-write race window. Fix: (1) Inside the runTransaction, before writing the appointment doc, write a deterministic reservation doc: slot_reservations/{date}_{slotHour}_{slotMin}_{department} (e.g., slot_reservations/2026-05-10_09_00_veterinary). Use transaction.set() with no merge — if the doc already exists from a concurrent booking, the transaction retries and the second client's retry finds the conflict. (2) The reservation doc schema: { ownerId, petId, appointmentId, createdAt, services[], expiresAt }. Lightweight — just enough to detect conflict. (3) On successful appointment write, the reservation doc persists (it IS the lock). On appointment cancellation or reschedule, delete the reservation doc (updateDoc path in BookAppointment.js reschedule + ClientAppointments.js cancel). (4) Reservation docs are keyed by slot start time + department. Multiple departments in the same booking write one reservation per department. The transaction.set() for each department reservation acts as a per-department mutex. (5) Cleanup: expired/orphaned reservations (from abandoned transactions) cleaned up by checking expiresAt — set to createdAt + 24 hours. The booking engine's slot computation can ignore reservations with expiresAt < now. (6) Firestore rules: slot_reservations — allow read: if isAuth(); allow create: if isAuth(); allow delete: if isAuth(). (7) Update useBookingEngine.js slot computation (effect 1, now onSnapshot) to also read slot_reservations for the selected day and treat them as occupied slots — prevents the slot grid from showing reserved slots as available. (8) The JIT pre-flight getDocs check can be REMOVED — the transaction-internal reservation write replaces it entirely. The transaction retries on contention, so no separate pre-flight needed. Files: BookAppointment.js (transaction.set reservation docs, remove JIT pre-flight), ClientAppointments.js (delete reservation on cancel), useBookingEngine.js (read reservations in slot computation), firestore.rules (slot_reservations collection). | P2 | 1.5 hrs | T2.87 | DONE | Closes the TOCTOU race condition. Deterministic doc ID pattern (YYYY-MM-DD_HH_MM_dept) means two concurrent bookings for the same slot collide inside the transaction — one succeeds, one retries and finds the conflict. JIT pre-flight getDocs removed from both submitBooking and submitReschedule. Reservation cleanup on client cancel (ClientAppointments.js) and admin cancel/reschedule (useQueueActions.js). dayReservations listener in useBookingEngine.js merges reservation ranges into slot computation. Firestore rules added. Same pattern used by Google Calendar and commercial booking systems. |
| T4.206 | AI Booking Advisor — mobile bottom sheet chat for intelligent booking guidance — Pet owners currently navigate a 4-step booking wizard with no personalized guidance. They must manually check which vaccines are due, remember recheck dates, and guess which time slots are best. Add an AI-powered booking advisor accessible via a Sky Blue "Ask AI" FAB on the BookAppointment screen. Opens a bottom sheet (same pattern as PetHistoryAISheet T4.97) with multi-turn chat powered by Claude Haiku via the existing Cloudflare Worker POST / endpoint. (1) NEW BookingAISheet component — bottom sheet with chat bubbles, ReactMarkdown, auto-scroll, input bar, always-visible. Sky Blue theme matching CalendarAIPanel. 3 quick chips: "What does my pet need?" (queries pet's vaccine status + recheck dates + overdue items), "Best time this week?" (queries slot availability from useBookingEngine), "Help me choose services" (queries services catalog filtered by pet species). (2) Booking context builder — formats for the system prompt: selected pet's vaccination status (from buildVaccinationStatus), recent medical history summary (last 3 records: diagnosis, discharge, recheck dates), pet profile (species, breed, age, weight, allergies), available services (filtered by species), clinic hours + closed dates, current step in wizard + already-selected services. One-shot getDocs for medical_records where petId matches (same pattern as CW dose auto-detect T4.200 Day 1). (3) System prompt — stored at system_prompts/booking_assistant, falls back to hardcoded DEFAULT_BOOKING_AI_PROMPT. Constrains to booking/scheduling topics. Blocks clinical diagnosis. Suggests services based on pet's status. Recommends dates based on clinic availability. Uses pet-owner-friendly language (not clinical jargon). (4) Integration with BookAppointment.js — FAB button visible on all 4 steps (position: absolute, bottom-right). Opens BookingAISheet with current context (selected pet, selected services, selected date). Context refreshes when pet/services/date change. (5) Mobile chatWithHistory — reuse the pattern from PetHistoryAISheet (fetch to Worker URL, parse response). No new llmService needed — PetHistoryAISheet already calls the Worker directly. (6) Audit logging — write to llm_audit_logs with source:'booking', petId, staffId (owner's UID). Same fire-and-forget pattern as PetHistoryAISheet. (7) Read-only intelligence — AI suggests services and times. Owner still books through the normal wizard. No autonomous booking. (8) No rate limits. Files: NEW BookingAISheet.js (mobile component), BookAppointment.js (FAB + sheet state + context builder), system_prompts/booking_assistant Firestore doc (optional, falls back to default). No Worker changes. | T4.207 | Bulk promo notification system — compose + preview + send to all opted-in clients — Staff currently sends notifications one client at a time via SendNotificationDialog. (2) Dashboard ReminderWidget: add "PROMOTIONAL" section with opted-in client count + "COMPOSE PROMO" button + "Last sent" date. Same location as vaccine/balance send buttons. (3) NotificationLogs page: add "COMPOSE PROMO" button in header + "Promos" filter tab alongside existing Appointments/Vaccines/Balance tabs. (4) Push always sent. Email and SMS are opt-in checkboxes per blast — staff controls cost (SMS is per-message). (5) "Save as Template" stores to promo_templates collection for future reuse. Template picker added to compose step when saved templates exist. (6) Full preview shows scrollable list of recipient names before send. Count confirmation: "Send to 47 clients. Cannot be undone." (7) 1-hour cooldown after successful blast — button disabled with countdown timer. Prevents accidental double-sends. (8) Filter: allowPromos === true only. No species/date sub-filters for v1. (9) All promo notifications logged to notification_log with type:'promo'. Bulk sends create one log entry per recipient (same pattern as vaccine reminders). | T4.208 | Referral field unification — merge referredBy + referralSource into structured referral object — Two separate scalar fields serve overlapping purposes: referredBy (person name, admin-only, T2.136) and referralSource (channel, mobile-only, T2.500). Staff asks "who referred you?" on admin, client selects "how did you find us?" on mobile. Different fields, different surfaces, no cross-surface visibility. Fix: (1) Unified data model: referral: { source: string, referredBy: string|null }. source is the channel (Walk-by/Facebook/Google/Referral/Returning/Vet Referral/Other). referredBy is the person name, only populated when source is "Referral" or "Vet Referral". (2) Mobile RegisterScreen: replace nothing (currently has no referral field) with chip selector for 7 source options + conditional "Referred by" TextInput. (3) Admin NewClientModal: replace "Referred by (Optional)" free-text TextField with source dropdown + conditional "Referred by" TextField. (4) Mobile UserProfileScreen: replace referralSource toggle chips with unified 7-option chips + conditional referredBy input. (5) Admin ClientDetails: update "Lead Source" select options to match 7 unified options + keep "Referred By" DataField conditional on source. (6) Admin ClientHeader chip: show "source · person" when both exist (e.g., "Ref: Maria Santos · Referral"). (7) usePatientManager: update editForm defaults + handleSaveProfile to write referral object. (8) useErasureEngine: null the referral object on erasure. (9) Backward compat: dual-read (referral.source || referralSource, referral.referredBy || referredBy) on all surfaces. Write BOTH new structured field AND legacy scalars for existing consumers. 13 touchpoints across 7 files. Files: RegisterScreen.js, UserProfileScreen.js, NewClientModal.jsx, ClientDetails.jsx, ClientHeader.jsx, usePatientManager.js, useErasureEngine.js. | P3 | 1 hr | — | DONE | Unified referral: { source, referredBy } object across 7 files. UserProfileScreen: replaced referralSource state with referral object, load with dual-read fallback, save with dual-write. NewClientModal: referral state object replaces form.referredBy, resetForms updated, ownerPayload dual-writes, JSX source Select + conditional TextField. ClientDetails: Lead Source 4→7 options with cascade clear of referredBy when source changes, Referred By DataField conditional on source. ClientHeader: chip IIFE with dual-read, combined "Ref: {person} · {source}" format. usePatientManager: referral default in editForm, dual-read load in handleSelectClient, dual-write in handleSaveProfile. useErasureEngine: referral: null alongside legacy scalars. | T4.187 | Expenses page upgrade — header consistency, date range, configurable categories with budgets, recurring repeat, export, expanded KPIs — Expenses.jsx is Intermediate-tier: single-day view only, filters in a mid-page bar (breaks consistency with all other admin pages), hardcoded 6 categories, no date range, no budget tracking, no recurring expense support, no export, KPIs are day-scoped only. 8 fixes: HEADER CONSISTENCY: (1) Move date picker + category filter chips from the mid-page row into the page header (Row 2 per T4.133 header unification pattern). Date range pickers + category dropdown + record count in the header alongside the title "EXPENSES" + "LOG EXPENSE" primary action button. KPI strip stays below header. Matches Queue, Inventory, Services, Staff, Transactions page layout. DATE RANGE: (2) Replace single date picker with start/end date range pickers + quick chips: Today, This Week, This Month, This Quarter. The onSnapshot query changes from single-day bounds to range bounds (startOfRange → endOfRange). Default: This Month (not today — monthly view is more useful for expense analysis). KPI EXPANSION: (3) KPIs update to reflect the selected period range: "PERIOD TOTAL" (replacing "DAY TOTAL"), "DAILY AVERAGE" (total ÷ days in range), "TOP CATEGORY" (keep), "ENTRIES" (keep), NEW "BUDGET STATUS" KPI showing aggregate spend vs aggregate budget for the period ("₱45,000 of ₱65,000 budget · 69%"). Color-coded: green < 75%, orange 75-90%, red > 90%. CONFIGURABLE CATEGORIES: (4) Replace hardcoded category chips (Utilities, Payroll, Supplies, Maintenance, Refunds, Other) with dynamic categories from a Firestore collection expense_categories (same pattern as inventory_categories). "Manage Categories" button on the Expenses page opens a dialog with: category name TextField + monthly budget TextField (₱) + Add button + list of existing categories with edit/delete IconButtons. Default seed: 6 current categories with ₱0 budget (no budget = no tracking). Each category doc: { name, monthlyBudget, isDefault, createdAt }. Firestore rules: expense_categories — allow read: isAuth(), allow create/update/delete: isStaff(). Filter chips on the page dynamically render from the collection. Decision 1: B — inline on Expenses page. BUDGET TRACKING: (5) Each category has a monthlyBudget field (from Decision 3: B). When viewing a monthly period, show per-category budget progress: "Utilities: ₱5,000 / ₱8,000 (62%)" with a mini progress bar. Color-coded: green < 75%, orange 75-90%, red > 90%. Rendered as a compact section below the KPI strip or as a collapsible "BUDGET STATUS" panel. Non-monthly periods (week, quarter) pro-rate the monthly budget proportionally. Categories with ₱0 budget show no progress bar (budget tracking opt-in per category). RECURRING EXPENSES: (6) Add isRecurring boolean flag on expense docs. When logging an expense, a "Recurring monthly" Switch in the add/edit dialog marks it. "REPEAT LAST MONTH" button in the page header (secondary action, outlined) — visible when viewing current month. onClick: queries last month's expenses where isRecurring === true, shows a confirmation dialog listing all recurring entries with amounts ("Copy 4 recurring expenses from April to May? Total: ₱35,000"), on confirm creates new expense docs for current month with same category/description/amount + loggedBy:'System (Repeat)' + sourceExpenseId pointing to the original. Idempotent: if current month already has repeated entries (check sourceExpenseId), show "Already repeated for this month" instead. Decision 2: B — manual trigger with review. EXPORT: (7) Add "EXPORT CSV" button (secondary action, outlined, same pattern as Inventory T3.27) in the page header. Exports filtered expenses for the selected date range as CSV: Date, Category, Description, Amount, Logged By. Add "PRINT REPORT" button — generates printable HTML summary grouped by category with totals per category + grand total + period header. Same printWindow pattern as Inventory/Sales. COLUMN IMPROVEMENTS: (8) Add "LOGGED BY" column to the DataGrid showing the staff member who created the entry. Add "UPDATED" column (conditional) showing updatedAt timestamp + updatedBy name if the expense was edited — audit visibility. Sort by date descending default. Decisions locked: B (inline category management), B ("Repeat Last Month" manual trigger), B (per-category budget alongside category manager). Skip: receipt attachment upload (Blaze-gated), approval workflow (overkill for 1-2 person clinic, T4.154 all staff full access). Files: Expenses.jsx (header layout, date range, KPIs, budget panel, repeat button, export, columns), NEW expense_categories Firestore collection + seed logic, firestore.rules (expense_categories collection rules). | P2 | 4-5 hrs | — | DONE | 8 fixes: header Row 1+2 (T4.133 pattern), date range with quick chips (Today/Week/Month/Quarter, default This Month, Manila timezone), 5 KPIs (PERIOD TOTAL/DAILY AVG/TOP CATEGORY/ENTRIES/BUDGET STATUS), expense_categories collection (onSnapshot + auto-seed 6 defaults + Manage Categories Dialog with name+monthlyBudget), budget progress bars (collapsible, 75%/90% color thresholds, pro-rated), isRecurring Switch + REPEAT LAST MONTH (idempotent via sourceExpenseId), EXPORT CSV (BOM) + PRINT REPORT (category-grouped HTML), LOGGED BY + UPDATED columns. 14/14 spec items verified. Operational completeness — expenses are the cost side of the P&L. Single-day view with hardcoded categories keeps it at Intermediate. Date range + configurable categories with budgets + recurring repeat + export moves it to Professional. Budget tracking per category enables "are we overspending on supplies?" without a spreadsheet. Repeat Last Month eliminates re-typing rent/utilities every month. Header consistency matches every other admin page. |
| T4.188 | Philippine veterinary legal compliance — regulatory fields on printed records, dual print option (client/internal), BAI vaccination fields, staff PRC/PTR — Printed medical records and vaccination certificates are missing Philippine regulatory fields required by RA 9268 (Veterinary Medicine Act), RA 9482 (Anti-Rabies Act), and PRC requirements. The clinical documentation content is thorough (SOAP, structured diagnosis, vitals, labs, prescriptions) but professional identification is absent from all printed documents. Currently: vet PRC license collected in StaffFormModal but never printed on any document, PTR number not collected anywhere, clinic BAI registration number not stored, owner address + phone not on visit summary, vaccination record has blank signature line but no auto-filled vet identity, no BAI Form No. 2 field compliance, only one print format (no client vs internal distinction). STAFF PROFILE FIELDS: (1) Add ptrNumber TextField to StaffFormModal.jsx (alongside existing prcLicense field). Label: "PTR Number" with helper text "Professional Tax Receipt — renewed annually". Optional, defaults to empty. Write to staff user doc. (2) Ensure prcLicense is written to staff doc on save (already collected, verify it persists — StaffFormModal line 33). CLINIC SETTINGS FIELDS: (3) Add baiRegistrationNumber TextField to Settings.jsx Clinic tab. Label: "BAI Registration Number" with helper text "Bureau of Animal Industry clinic registration". Optional, defaults to empty. Write to clinic_settings/general. Add to useClinicSettings DEFAULT_SETTINGS as empty string. VISIT SUMMARY PRINT (printVisitSummary.js): (4) Add to the printed header: clinic phone (clinicSettings.clinicPhone — already in useClinicSettings), clinic BAI registration (new field). (5) Add owner address + phone below owner name: read from owner doc (user.address, user.city, user.phone). Requires passing owner object to generateVisitSummaryHTML (currently only passes owner name). Fallback: "Address: Not provided" if empty. (6) Add vet regulatory block below "Attending Vet" line: "PRC License: {prcLicense}" + "PTR: {ptrNumber}". Requires looking up the vet's staff doc by assignedVetId to read prcLicense + ptrNumber. Fallback: "PRC: —" if not filled. (7) Add blank signature line at the bottom: "_____ Veterinarian Signature" + "_____ Date Signed" (matching vaccination record pattern). Vet signs physically with pen. Decision 1: B — blank line, auto-apply as future enhancement. VACCINATION RECORD PRINT (printVaccinationRecord.js): (8) Update existing template with BAI-required fields — do NOT create a separate BAI Form No. 2 template. Decision 2: B. Add: owner address (user.address + user.city), owner phone (user.phone), clinic BAI registration number, vet PRC license + PTR number per vaccine entry (from assignedVet lookup), clinic phone. (9) Auto-fill the currently blank "Veterinarian Name & License No." signature cell with: vet full name + "PRC: {prcLicense}" + "PTR: {ptrNumber}" from the vet who administered each vaccine (vaccineAdministrations[].administeredBy or record.vetName → look up staff doc). Signature line stays blank for physical signing. DUAL PRINT OPTION: (10) Replace the single "Print" button on PatientDashboard per-record with a dropdown menu button: "PRINT ▾" → menu with 3 options: "Client Copy", "Internal Copy", "Both (2 pages)". Decision 3: B — dropdown menu. (11) Client Copy — current printVisitSummary output enhanced with regulatory fields (steps 4-7). Plain language: "Discharge Notes" not "Plan", diagnosis in non-clinical terms where possible, medications with dosing instructions, follow-up date, clinic contact. Designed for the pet owner to take home. (12) Internal Copy — NEW template (generateInternalRecordHTML or extend printVisitSummary with a mode parameter). Full SOAP with clinical terminology (S, O, A, P labels), all vitals (7 fields), complete diagnosis list with severity + category, full prescription list with qty + sig + staff attribution, amendment history (structured + legacy), pulse timeline summary (key events: arrival, consult start, sign-off, checkout), forensic metrics (record age, queue time, consult time), service list with per-service attribution + timestamps, record ID, all regulatory fields (clinic BAI, vet PRC + PTR, owner full address + phone). Designed for clinic filing and BAI inspection. (13) "Both" option generates a 2-page document: page 1 = client copy, page 2 = internal copy with CSS page-break-before. One print dialog, two copies. (14) Same dropdown on Records page (Visit Log) audit popover print button — currently only has a single print icon. Also on ClinicalWorkspace if a print action exists there. GRACEFUL FALLBACKS: All new regulatory fields are optional with graceful empty states — "PRC: —", "PTR: —", "BAI: Not configured", "Address: Not provided". The system SUPPORTS compliance without ENFORCING it. Printed records are complete and functional even when regulatory fields are empty — they just have labeled blank spaces that prompt the clinic to fill in their profiles. No workflow gates, no blocking dialogs. Decisions locked: B (blank signature line, physical signing), B (update existing vaccination template with BAI fields), B (one button with dropdown: Client/Internal/Both). Files: StaffFormModal.jsx (add ptrNumber field), useStaffManager.js (save ptrNumber), Settings.jsx (add baiRegistrationNumber), useClinicSettings.js (add to defaults), printVisitSummary.js (add regulatory fields + owner address/phone + vet PRC/PTR + signature line), printVaccinationRecord.js (add BAI fields + auto-fill vet identity), NEW generateInternalRecordHTML.js (or mode param on existing), PatientDashboard.jsx (dropdown print button), Records.jsx (dropdown print in audit popover). | P1 | 3-4 hrs | — | DONE | Day 1: ptrNumber on StaffFormModal + useStaffManager, baiRegistrationNumber on Settings + useClinicSettings, printVisitSummary enhanced (clinic phone/BAI, owner address, vet PRC/PTR via UID lookup, blank signature lines), printVaccinationRecord enhanced (BAI, owner address, per-row vet PRC/PTR via staffLookup Map, certification auto-fill), PatientDashboard print callers updated (buildStaffLookup UID-first). | T4.189 | Visit Log phase-aware action buttons — bring Queue workflow actions into the Visit Log page for all records regardless of date — The Visit Log (Records.jsx) currently has only 3 action buttons per row (Audit, CRM, Print). Staff must navigate to Queue to perform workflow actions (Check In, Start Consult, Open Workspace, Verify Dispensing, Checkout, Revert). This forces a context switch and only works for today's records — yesterday's completed visit that needs reverting requires Firestore Console. Fix: add phase-aware action buttons to the Visit Log DataGrid that mirror the Queue's status-gated actions, operating on appointment documents by ID (date-independent). STATUS→ACTION MAPPING (same as Queue queueColumns.jsx lines 698-850): (1) pending → "Accept" (→confirmed) + "Defer" button + overflow menu (Cancel, No-Show, Reschedule). (2) confirmed → "Check In" button (opens AssignStaffModal) + overflow menu (Cancel, No-Show, Reschedule). Case day >1 shows "RE-ARRIVE & RESUME". (3) arrived → "Start Consult" button (→in-consult) + overflow menu. Case day >1 shows "RESUME". (4) in-consult/confined/on-hold → "Workspace" button (opens ClinicalWorkspace) + overflow menu (Put On Hold, Resume). (5) dispensing → "Verify" button (opens DispensingVerificationDialog) or "Resolve Hold" if dispensingHold. (6) billing → "Checkout" button (opens POSModal) + overflow menu. (7) completed/carried-over/cancelled/no-show (terminal) → "Revert" button with mandatory auditReason TextField (same revertStatusWithReason pattern from Queue.jsx lines 992-1003). IMPLEMENTATION: (8) Import useQueueActions hook into Records.jsx — already exports changeStatus, revertStatus, markNoShow, rejectAppointment, quickAdmitER, deferAppointment, rescheduleAppointment. All operate on appointment docs by ID — zero date dependency. (9) Add modal state + render for: AssignStaffModal (check-in flow), ClinicalWorkspace (consult), POSModal (checkout), DispensingVerificationDialog (dispensing verify). These are the same components already used on Queue — import and render with the selected row as patient prop. (10) Add overflow menu state (anchorEl + selectedRow) for per-row context menus — Cancel, No-Show, Reschedule, Defer, Put On Hold, Resume. Same Menu pattern as Queue. (11) Replace the current 3-icon action column (Audit, CRM, Print at width:160) with a wider action column (width:280) that renders the phase-aware primary button + overflow IconButton + Audit IconButton + CRM IconButton + Print IconButton. Primary button changes per status. Audit + CRM + Print stay on all rows. (12) Revert button for terminal statuses: show on COMPLETED tab rows. MUI Dialog with auditReason TextField (mandatory, same as Queue). Calls revertStatus from useQueueActions. Works on ANY completed/cancelled/no-show record regardless of date — the Firestore updateDoc operates by document ID. (13) PENDING tab: primary actions stay (Accept/Defer) alongside the existing checkbox bulk selection. Checkbox selects rows for bulk operations; action buttons operate on individual rows. No conflict — same pattern as Queue. (14) The action buttons must be compact — use the same btnStyle pattern from queueColumns.jsx (fontWeight:1000, fontSize:0.65rem, height:28, borderRadius:0). Primary button gets flex:1, overflow + audit + CRM + print are fixed-width IconButtons. (15) Inventory data: POSModal and DispensingVerificationDialog need joinedInventory. Add inventory + categories listeners to Records.jsx (same pattern as Sales.jsx T4.184 — 3 onSnapshot listeners with cleanup). servicesList also needed for ClinicalWorkspace — add services listener. DATA INDEPENDENCE: All useQueueActions handlers write to Firestore appointment documents by ID. They do NOT filter by date, do NOT require the appointment to be in today's queue, and do NOT read from daily_queue. A 3-week-old completed appointment can be reverted to billing, then checked out again via POSModal. The only date-sensitive action is "Check In" for confirmed appointments — which creates a queue position for today. This is correct behavior: checking in a rescheduled appointment should place it in today's queue. Decisions needed: None — all actions mirror existing Queue behavior exactly. The only question is column width (240 vs 280 vs separate action row). Files: Records.jsx (action column expansion, modal state + render, useQueueActions import, inventory/services listeners, overflow menu), useQueueActions.js (no changes — already exported). Possibly extract a shared ActionButtons component if the renderCell becomes too large. | P2 | 3-4 hrs | T4.183 | DONE | Day 1: useQueueActions expanded (6 handlers), inventory/categories/services listeners + joinedInventory, useClosingStatus, action column 160→280 with status-gated primary buttons (Accept/Check In/Start Consult/Workspace/Verify/Checkout/Revert) + overflow Menu + Audit/CRM/Print, Revert + No-Show dialogs with mandatory reason. Day 2: 4 modal renders (AssignStaffModal, ClinicalWorkspace, POSModal, DispensingVerificationDialog) with verified prop signatures. Full workflow from Visit Log — any historical record can be reverted, re-consulted, re-checked-out. Build clean, 322/322 tests pass. |
| T4.154 | Role simplification — remove admin/staff distinction, all clinic staff have full access — The admin/staff role split creates friction for the 1-2 vet PH target clinic where the same person does everything. Currently: 5 routes are AdminRoute-gated (Staff, Sales, Expenses, Settings, Reports), sidebar hides 5 items from non-admins, Firestore rules use isAdmin() for 16 destructive/sensitive operations, StaffFormModal has an accessLevel dropdown, UserContext computes isAdmin, QuickNavTiles filters adminOnly items, Queue restricts terminal revert to admins, Dashboard report PDF gates Financial tab on isAdmin. Fix: (1) Firestore rules — make isAdmin() an alias for isStaff(): `function isAdmin() { return isStaff(); }`. One-line change, all 16 existing isAdmin() calls continue working. Also fix getUserRole() by reverting to simple get().data (removes the exists() pattern that's causing evaluation failures). Also add accessLevel fallback to isStaff(): d.role OR d.accessLevel in allowed list. (2) App.jsx — delete AdminRoute component (lines 81-83), unwrap all 5 routes from AdminRoute wrappers (Staff, Sales, Expenses, Settings, Reports become direct Route elements). Remove isAdmin from useUser() destructure. (3) Sidebar.jsx — remove adminOnly:true from 5 menu items (Staff, Transactions, Expenses, Settings, Forensic Reports). Remove isAdmin import + visibleMenuItems filter. All staff see all items. (4) UserContext.jsx — keep isAdmin computed value but document it as always-true for all staff (or remove it). Consumers that reference isAdmin still work — they just always get true. (5) QuickNavTiles.jsx — remove adminOnly:true from Sales/Expenses tiles, remove isAdmin filtering. (6) StaffFormModal.jsx — remove the accessLevel Select dropdown. All new staff get role:'staff' (or inherit from formData without the choice). The field can default to 'staff' silently. (7) useStaffManager.js line 180 — keep role: 'staff' as the default for all new staff. Remove accessLevel from the payload or keep it as 'staff' for backward compat. (8) Queue.jsx line 2127 — remove the isAdmin guard on terminal revert. All staff can revert terminal statuses. (9) generateReportHTML.js — remove isAdmin parameter, always include Financial tab. (10) Settings.jsx — remove isAdmin check if any. Deploy Firestore rules after commit. Files: firestore.rules, App.jsx, Sidebar.jsx, UserContext.jsx, QuickNavTiles.jsx, StaffFormModal.jsx, useStaffManager.js, Queue.jsx, generateReportHTML.js, Settings.jsx. | P2 | 1.5-2 hrs | — | DONE | UX simplification — the admin/staff distinction is friction for the target 1-2 vet PH clinic. Also permanently fixes the getUserRole() Firestore rules regression and the role/accessLevel dual-field confusion (T4.81). |

| T4.98 | Centralized status transition function — Currently 19 status write paths across 5 files (useQueueActions.js, Queue.jsx, AssignStaffModal.jsx, ClinicalWorkspace.jsx, POSModal.jsx) each independently reimplement the statusHistory + clinicalPulse + auditReasons + sendPushNotification pattern. Supports both runTransaction and writeBatch contexts via an adapter pattern. ~8-10 hrs across 5 files. | P3 | 8-10 hrs | — | TODO | Architectural — prevents future statusHistory/pulse/push gaps. Every new status path would go through one gate instead of reimplementing 4 patterns. |
| T4.99 | Client-side timestamp validation via Firestore rules — All clinicalPulse timestamps use client-side Timestamp.now() (W1 weakness in pulseUtils.js). A device with a wrong clock corrupts the audit timeline. Fix: add a Firestore security rule on the appointments collection that validates pulse event timestamps against server time: reject writes where any new clinicalPulse event's timestamp differs from request.time by more than ±5 minutes. Firestore rules have access to request.time (server-side) for comparison. This does NOT require Blaze — pure security rules. Also add a client-side warning in the admin app if the device clock differs from server time by >2 minutes (compare Date.now() against a Firestore server timestamp probe on app load). ~2-3 hrs. | P3 | 2-3 hrs | — | TODO | Data integrity — prevents corrupt audit timelines from misconfigured device clocks without requiring Cloud Functions |

Add a usePermissions() hook that reads the current user's role/accessLevel and the clinic permission config. Gate sensitive actions: clinical sign-off (vet/admin only), staff management (admin only), terminal status changes (admin/vet only), inventory adjustments (admin/staff only). UI: hide or disable buttons/menu items for unauthorized roles. Settings page: permission matrix editor for the admin. ~6-8 hrs. | P3 | 6-8 hrs | T4.81 | TODO | Security — role separation prevents unauthorized clinical actions. Mobile PetHistoryScreen: departments one-shot getDocs, filterOptions useMemo with fallback, department color on active chip, defensive reset. |
| T4.108 | Fix AI markdown table rendering in ClinicalWorkspace — The AI clinical reasoning output shows raw markdown table syntax (pipe characters and \|---\|---\|---\| separator rows) instead of rendered HTML tables. The ClinicalWorkspace DiagnosticBridge component uses react-markdown with custom components map (table, th, td renderers at ~line 413-423) but the table delimiter row renders as raw text. Likely cause: line break formatting in the LLM response doesn't match react-markdown's expected table syntax (needs blank line before table, consistent pipe alignment). Fix: either pre-process the AI response to normalize table formatting before passing to ReactMarkdown, or add a regex-based table cleaner that ensures blank lines surround tables and delimiter rows are properly formatted. Test with the "Analyze S+O" output which consistently produces differential diagnosis tables. ~30 min. | P2 | 30 min | — | DONE | normalizeMarkdownTables.js utility (blank line injection, trailing pipe, cell padding, delimiter row normalization). Applied to ClinicalWorkspace DiagnosticBridge + PetHistoryAIDrawer AI messages. ReactMarkdown components map untouched. |
| T4.109 | SOAP quadrant swap: S+O left column, A+P right column — ClinicalWorkspace currently renders the 2x2 SOAP grid as [S\|O / A\|P] (Subjective top-left, Objective top-right, Assessment bottom-left, Plan bottom-right). This layout forces the vet to read S (top-left) then jump to O (top-right), then jump back to A (bottom-left) — diagonal eye movement. Fix: swap to [S\|A / O\|P] so the left column is reference data (S + O stacked) and the right column is writing fields (A + P stacked). The vet reads the complaint + findings on the left, writes the diagnosis + plan on the right — natural left-to-right clinical workflow. Apply to BOTH the default SoapGrid (inside the ClinicalWorkspace Dialog) AND the God View SoapGrid (inside the isUnifiedZen Dialog). The SoapGrid component renders the quadrant order — the swap is a reordering of the grid children, not a layout restructure. Also convert the ClinicalWorkspace AI from single-shot (analyzeSoap) to multi-turn (chatWithHistory from T4.96) — the Analyze S+O and Ask AI buttons stay in the Assessment quadrant but responses now support follow-up questions. The multi-turn state (messages array) persists within the ClinicalWorkspace session and resets on close/patient change. ~3-4 hrs. | P1 | 3-4 hrs | T4.108 | DONE | SoapGrid quadrant swap: S top-left, A top-right, O bottom-left, P bottom-right. buildUserMessage exported from llmService. Multi-turn AI: llmMessages[] replaces llmResponse, chatWithHistory replaces callClinicalReasoning, 20-msg sliding window, follow-up InputBase in DiagnosticBridge, "New Analysis" reset button, LLM state resets on patient change. |
| T4.110 | AI side panel: collapsible drawer in default view, persistent panel in God View — Add an AI conversation panel to the ClinicalWorkspace that the vet can reference while writing. Default view: a toggleable right-side MUI Drawer (same pattern as T4.96 PetHistoryAIDrawer) that overlays the Services sidebar when open. Collapsible — hidden by default, toggled via the existing Analyze S+O / Ask AI buttons (which now open the drawer instead of rendering inline). Contains the multi-turn chat from T4.109 with quick-action chips ("Analyze S+O", "Suggest Differentials", "Recommend Diagnostics"). God View: the AI panel is a persistent non-collapsible third column on the right side of the SOAP grid (~30% width). The God View layout changes from 2-column [S+O \| A+P] to 3-column [S+O \| A+P \| AI]. The AI panel shares the same messages state as the default view drawer — switching between views preserves the conversation. Both surfaces use ReactMarkdown with the same components map as DiagnosticBridge (table, headings, lists). System prompt uses the current SOAP data as context (rebuilt on each send via the existing buildUserMessage pattern). ~3-4 hrs. | P1 | 3-4 hrs | T4.109 | DONE | ClinicalAIPanel.jsx shared component (variant drawer/column, rule-based + LLM conversation, quick-action chips, follow-up input, normalizeMarkdownTables). DiagnosticBridge slimmed to buttons-only (~280→80 lines). Default view: 420px MUI Drawer overlay. God View: 3-column [SoapGrid flex:7 | AI Panel flex:3]. SoapGrid: 6 LLM display props removed, 2 button props added. Conversation state shared across views. |
